 **EFFORT IS A FEATURE, NOT A BUG [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** **voting should require
 some effort** -- **not show up and check boxes.** Effort filters **indifference** without
 filtering **capacity**, which is exactly what a ballot should do.
 **What "effort" means here [and what it must not mean]:**
 | Effort type | Keep / reject | Why |
 |-------------|---------------|-----|
 | **Show up in person, on the day** | **KEEP** | removes drive-by voting; a person who will not travel has not decided |
 | **Fill a complete ballot [no abstain]** | **KEEP** | forces a position on every filed name [MANDATORY NET-SCORE] |
 | **Know basic civics** | **KEEP** [civic pass] | facts, not opinion |
 | **Read the ledger you are voting on** | **KEEP** -- **one-page summary at the poll**, not a library | the number is the thing being steered |
 | **Pay a fee** | **REJECT** | wealth gate [NO FEE ON OVERSIGHT rhyme; POOR IMMOBILITY [c]] |
 | **Own property / pay net tax as a *separate* bar** | **already the meter** -- do not double | one pay-in test, not two |
 | **Long forms, confusing instructions, distant hours** | **REJECT** | that is **friction**, not **effort** -- it selects by patience and slack, not by decision |
 **The distinction that matters:** **effort = a deliberate act a decided person will
 perform.** **Friction = an obstacle that catches decided and undecided alike.** A queue of
 four hours is **friction** and falls on people with the least flexible time
 [POOR IMMOBILITY]; a requirement to appear, mark every line, and pass a baby civic bank is
 **effort** and costs a decided person an afternoon.
 **Falsification:** any added requirement whose **burden falls on people already trying to
 vote** rather than on people **not trying** = friction dressed as effort = failed. A change
 that **raises turnout among the indifferent** without raising decision quality = effort
 failed. Effort that **scales with wealth or free time** = friction = failed.
 **Delivery language = English only** [OFFICIAL ENGLISH AND MINIMUM LANGUAGE].

 **Fed guidelines / state machines:** **Federal body** [agency or board under
 statute] pins **fact bank**, **pass score**, **security floor**, **English
 delivery**; **states** build **good** tests and admin inside guidelines --
 scheduling, proctors, forms. **Substance pinned**; admin may vary by locality.
 **Bad testing** [off-bank softball, leaked keys, sabotage scoring, political
 circus below federal floor] = **cheating** -- **same $1,000,000 fine tier** +
 franchise bar as test-taker fraud [symmetric cheat religion -- both ends].

 **Secure delivery:** identity at sit; proctored or hardened digital;
 auditable pass/fail. **Cheating** [impersonation, answer purchase, proctor
 compromise, system fraud] = **$1,000,000 fine** tier + franchise bar while
 open [rhyme TAX AXIOMS [7] felony stack].

 **Franchise tie:** **Active civic pass** required for **Roll B ballot** that
 cycle. **Fail** attempt = **no vote ~90 days** [cooling-off], **retake**
 unlimited -- **not** permanent strip. **Pass** = may vote **only if** Roll B
 net-positive also holds [B already policy -- total ledger, no pass payout].

 **Naturalization:** USCIS-aligned civics pass **satisfies** until statutory
 refresh [if any -- default **one-time** unless congress pins renewal].

 **COMPETENCE SCALES WITH THE POWER [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** one baby bank cannot
 gate a ballot **and** a judge-selection panel **and** a compel petition **and** a death
 trial. **The easy test is correct for voting and wrong for judging.** So the bar **tiers
 with what the seat can do:**
 | Power | Bar | Why |
 |-------|-----|-----|
 | **Vote** | civic pass [current baby bank] | filters indifference + basic orientation; **must stay easy** [effort ≠ difficulty] |
 | **Sortition review / compel petition** | civic pass **+ the enrolled rules of the row being judged**, open-book | they are **deciding one pinned question**, not running a state -- the material **is** the competence |
 | **Higher-court selection** | civic pass **+ written reason** for the pick, published | forces **logos on the record**; a pick that cannot state a reason is a jersey pick |
 | **Death tier / liberty verdict** | full trial process [jury + judge supermajority, long hold] | already the heaviest bar in the file -- **do not lower it** |
 **How the tier is applied without a "smart people" class:** the extra requirements are
 **open-book + row-specific**, never a **general intelligence or education screen**.
 The bar is **"do you know this case"**, not **"are you the right sort"** [rhyme
 COIN-ONLY GUARD: no virtue score; NO NUMBER FROM NOTHING: material is the source].
 **Mid filters, not elite filters:** the point is **not** to select for higher capability --
 it is to make **the specific decision legible to the person making it.** A mid who reads
 the row **clears** the bar; a credential-holder who will not read it **does not**.
 **Void:** an **education, IQ, or credential** requirement on any tier = **elite filter** =
 failed. A tier where the extra bar is **closed-book or unstated** = **guessing, not
 competence** = failed. Using the **death-tier process as a model for ordinary rows** =
 cost without benefit = failed.

 RECESSION NON-MUTE STACK [fix -- chat 2026-11-05; pay-to-play preserved]:
 **Bad nation = bad everything** -- a recession that mass-mutes the payer class is
 a **nation failure**, not a meter exception. Fix runs **upstream of the meter**:
 keep people **paying**, don't forgive them.
 [a] **Pre-funded floor reserve:** active pull collected **above** enrolled floor
 cost routes to a published **reserve line** in good years; recession draws the
 reserve to hold floor + work lanes. Collected pull spent later = pay-as-you-go
 **across time**, **not** leverage -- **no debt row created** [recession-debt
 prohibition [5] untouched; nation-death exception untouched].
 **[a1] RESERVE FLOOR = PEOPLE'S LIVING COST ON REAL DATA [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:**
 what counts as **"above floor cost"** is **not a central estimate**, **not a dial**, and
 **not a state-set number**. **People set it by living and by minimum stuff** -- the floor
 is the **cost of the minimum basket actually needed to live where they live**, measured
 on **enrolled data**:
 - **Basis = minimum-stuff basket priced where people actually live**, using the
 **existing enrolled series** [NECESSITY BASKET PIN TABLE: food, power, shelter,
 transport, care; RESOURCE PANEL METRIC PINS; ACS/BLS/EIA rows -- **no new survey, no
 modeled estimate**].
 - **Unit = person and household**, not a state average. **Each locality's floor is
 priced from the basket its residents face** [rhyme necessity basket local rebase
 rules]; the national line = **population-weighted aggregate of those local floors**.
 - **Reserve trigger** = collected pull **above** that computed floor line. Recomputed
 **annually from published prices**, never negotiated.
 - **Median, not mean** -- a mean is moved by the extracting tail and would raise the
 floor line to justify more collection.
 **WHY NOT STATE-MEDIANS [fix -- chat 2026-11-05]:** a **state median is an outcome of
 state policy**, so pricing the floor off state medians lets a **low-provision state drag
 its own median down**, lowering its reserve obligation -- **under-provision rewarded**.
 **The basket is not a state's choice; it is what a person has to buy to live.** Floor
 priced from **the basket + the prices people actually pay** cannot be written down by
 governing badly.
 **Why this closes the ratchet:** the floor cost cannot be **written down** to push more
 into reserve, because the line is **derived from published prices for a fixed basket**
 and judged on **the same panels that score success** [rhyme RESOURCE PANEL: the meter
 that scores also sets the charge].
 **Void / fail:** reserve accumulation while **any enrolled locality's basket cost
 outruns what the floor delivers** = floor defined too low = failed. Floor line set by
 **agency estimate or state median** instead of the published basket calculation =
 discretion re-entered = failed. **A locality cannot lower its floor by under-providing** --
 if it tries, the basket price stands and the gap shows on the panel = failed.
 **[a2] Reserve caps:** **published ceiling** set by the **same measured basis as the floor
 line** -- a stated **multiple of annual computed floor cost** [a1], with the multiple
 **pinned in statute and revised only on published data**, not by budget resolution.
 **No invented figure:** the ceiling is expressed as a **ratio to the computed floor**, so
 it scales automatically with real prices and never requires an author to guess a dollar
 amount. **Balance above ceiling returns** as rate reduction or floor improvement, **not**
 as further collection. **No indefinite accumulation** -- a permanently growing reserve
 is **a tax without a purpose** [rhyme no roll-debt OS: hoarding is not provision].
 [b] **Meter reads the cycle as filed:** no averaging, no memory, no carry-forward
 [NO MEMORY pin]. A bad year is a bad year for everyone -- **uniform**, which is
 the point; the reserve exists so fewer people have one.
 [c] **Reserve drawdown published quarterly** next to spend vs collected pull.
 **Falsification:** recession-year mute rate exceeds pre-recession baseline by more
 than the **enrolled mute-rate band** [MEASUREMENT PRECISION CEILING: **direction inside a
 band, not a point** -- the band is **calibrated from the observed mute-rate history on the
 same panels**, not chosen to fit a conclusion] while reserve line sits **undrawn** → stack
 failed [reserve was a poster, not a buffer]. **If no enrolled history exists yet, the
 first cycle's observed rate becomes the baseline** [stated openly -- no invented number
 on day zero]. Reserve drawn but floor-access panels worsen → failed.
 Reserve used to fund normal ops in a good year → failed [rhyme no roll-debt OS].
 **Reserve accumulating while an enrolled locality's basket cost outruns what the floor
 delivers** = floor computed too low = failed [a1]. **Floor line set by agency estimate or
 state median instead of the published basket calculation** = discretion re-entered =
 failed. **A locality under-providing while its floor obligation falls** = under-provision
 rewarded = failed. **Balance held
 above the published ceiling without a rate cut or floor improvement** = hoarding, not
 provision = failed [a2].

 **What redo does / does not:**
 **Does:** tie voice to **net pay-in** on SIMPLE FRANCHISE METER + **basic
 civic pass**; elevate fiscal and orientation stakes of elections; uniform
 eligibility membrane; pairs with **UNIVERSAL PERSON CAP** [DIRECT CONTRIBUTOR]
 on money voice.
 **Does not:** replace program-integrity enforcement; end debt; or map
 cleanly to R/D jerseys [many households flip sign by year, age, transfers].
 **Does not alone:** cap billionaire spend -- **install with** same-cap
 campaign-finance stack.

 sham marriage / unit-split gaming spike. **Signed arithmetic must hold:** every
 government benefit row appears **minus**, and **no cohort** carries a birth-year
 off-list [a benefit with a plus sign, or a band carve, = arithmetic fail].

 payment counted as a positive = loophole open = failed. **Child rule must hold:**
 a child-raising household reading **more negative** than an identical childless one
 = formation penalty = failed; a **refundable child credit paying out above tax
 paid** = transfer = failed; **child relief adding a plus** or **loosening the
 vote bar** = failed [relief shrinks the positive only]. **Pass lanes must hold:** severe physical incapacity
 muted = failed; "lazy / mentally ill" claimed as physical incapacity = fraud lane.

 PENSION / RETIREE COUNTERS [chat 2026-11-05 -- signed-arithmetic pass; analyst
 reply]:
 **[10] "Retiree paid in forty years and now can't vote":** **True on the meter,
 and that is the rule working, not failing.** Prior pay bought the **floor** and the
 floor is **unconditional** -- never revoked, never means-tested down. Voice is a
 **current-cycle** row, not a refund of past rows. **Desk accepts** the mute openly.
 **Rejoinder the desk does not run:** "they earned it" -- that is **return-class
 reasoning**, already superseded; it would also license every future client to buy
 a ballot in installments.
 **[11] "Gov support is a cost -- so benefits make you a client" -- agreed, that is
 the design:** **government benefits are a negative number, always** [SS, Medicare,
 disability, means-tested transfers]. They are **not useful** on the meter
 -- they are a **draw**. A retiree votes only when **dividends, profit, income,
 spending, or rent** on the **plus** side **outrun** the benefit minus. **Support ≠
 automatic mute, but support never counts for you** -- that is the whole arithmetic.
 **Benefit still stands** [floor unconditional]; **negative is not useful, still has
 benefits.** [signed arithmetic pin].
 **[12] "Proper profit proves median-or-above, so this is a wealth test":**
 **No -- and profit is the proof against it.** The bar is **net sign > $0**, not
 income percentile. A **correctly-reported** profit at the margin [tiny shop,
 small rental, modest surplus] clears Roll B at whatever income level it lands;
 a high earner whose benefit draw exceeds in **fails at the same line**. What
 profit proves is **transformation happening at the membrane** -- the spine's actual
 criterion [rhyme RESOURCE PANEL; open flat profit at org membrane]. **Positive =
 useful; negative = not useful, may still have benefits.** Wealth is not the
 classifier; **net sign is.**
 **[13] "Why punish pensioners when the problem is boomers":** **Agreed on the
 distinction -- and the stack already draws it.** The problem is **stolen-years
 substrate extraction**, not a generation of bodies; the 1946–1964 cohort is the
 **physical form** the pattern took, which is why the meter carries **no birth-year
 band**: one rule, every cohort, all benefits minus. **No cohort is exempted and
 none is singled out** -- the peak-incidence body **fails the arithmetic in bulk**,
 which is how the problem becomes visible and countable. Extra deprioritization
 [investment-dwelling sniff] stays where it is because it targets **asset
 position**, not age. **No permanent anti-old class:** the meter follows **current
 activity**, so anyone -- at any birth year, including the peak cohort -- who earns,
 spends, or profits above their draw votes.

OFFICE TENURE AND BRANCH JURISDICTION [analyst policy -- analyst chat
 2026-10-10]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst operative governance spec -- not

 FIXED SPEC [operative defaults -- band ranges only where compact context
 says otherwise]:
 - **Office age:** **55** → **immediate vacate** [elected, judicial, union
 commissioner picks]. **Cannot file** if term would run past 55.
 - **Franchise age:** **25** vote [AEC default]; compact may set **18**
 [CONTRIBUTOR LEDGER install context only].

 or age **55**, whichever **first**.

 - **Judicial recall on rulings:** **never**.

 clock**.

 - **Apex:** one **staggered** national panel per lane.

 **Age 55 [elected + judicial + union commissioner picks]:**
 **55th birthday** → **office vacant immediately** [no lame-duck extension].
 Special fill per statute. Goal: **no gerontocracy lock** on routing valves.

 **Judicial term + age:** **10 years total** or age **55** -- whichever comes
 **first** ends tenure. **No** reappointment, no elevation reward track.

 **Elected:** **recall** stays [quarterly ledger]; age cap is **hard** second
 exit -- not replaced by calendar terms.

 FOUR BRANCH LANES [exclusive jurisdiction -- case must land in one]:
 [1] **LEGISLATURE** -- statute, budget, tax **rates** inside active-only
 rules, war **declaration** class, charter amendment **proposal** [not
 final membrane ratify alone].
 [2] **EXECUTIVE** -- **execute** statute, operational foreign/military
 **within** legislative mandate, appointments **below** apex panels,
 agency run **with sunset** on regs; **no** election adjudication, **no**
 contributor topology cases.
 [3] **LAW COURT** -- criminal/civil disputes, contract, property **usage**,
 ordinary appeals; **not** franchise, **not** election process, **not**
 contributor-money topology, **not** compact membrane.
 [4] **MEMBRANE TRIBUNAL** -- **only**: election **process** [paper chain,
 rolls, custody], **contributor ledger + DIRECT CONTRIBUTOR CAMPAIGN
 FINANCE** enforcement, **franchise gate** disputes, **compact /
 membrane** interpretation; **quorum-proof** enforcement [no FEC-style
 deadlock kill switch on merits built].

 **Split control rule:** if issue touches **two** lanes [e.g. prosecution
 vs ballot access], **MEMBRANE** decides **process/franchise/money-topology**
 row; **LAW COURT** decides **ordinary crime** row; **LEGISLATURE** writes
 gap-fill statute -- **no** single omni-bench cert grab.

 **WHY NO JUDICIAL RECALL ON MERITS [load-bearing]:**
 Judges are the **law cage** -- short fixed term + age **55** + crime
 removal already bound them. **Merit recall** = mob or party **re-votes every
 unpopular ruling** → judges **pre-rig** toward jersey, donors, or panic --
 same failure mode as **SCOTUS neutrality theater** with extra steps. **Wrong
 ruling** → **appeal** inside correct branch + **pickers** who seated the
 panel lose seats; **crime** [bribery, fabrication] → instant remove. AEC
 separates **political recall** [executives] from **judicial predictability**
 [rules must bite even when losers hate the outcome].

 **JUDICIAL BIAS [allowed but limited -- operative]:**
 **Bias allowed:** priors, sophist register, analyst-style **labeled worldview**
 [no neutrality cosplay requirement]. Humans judge; pretending **zero bias**
 = **logos theater** [rhymes SCOTUS NEUTRALITY THEATER].
 **Bias limited [hard]:**
 [1] **Jurisdiction lane** -- cannot hear or decide outside owning branch
 table [no omni-grab to follow ideology].
 [2] **Conflict / gift** -- undisclosed financial or electioneering tie =
 crime panel [bias bought = not protected bias].
 [3] **Published reasoning** -- outcome + cited compact/statute row on
 ledger [concealed routing = fabrication class].
 [4] **Appeal** -- loser gets **branch-correct** review, not street veto.
 [5] **Term + age** -- **10 years or 55**; pickers may seat **different**
 school-foil next cycle [bias rotates, does not immortalize].
 **Not a removal ground alone:** ruling **you dislike** or **ideological
 drift** without crime/lane breach -- fix via appeal + **new pickers**, not
 merit recall.

 **CRITICAL ISSUE CLASS [four-branch binding actions]:**
 Compact amendment, war **declaration**, nation-band tax/membrane rewrite,
 election-process or **contributor-topology** change, emergency powers beyond
 sunset, L0 land-class flip. Statute pins list; legislature may add rows with
 **membrane tribunal** sign-off only.

 **TWO–TWO DEADLOCK [2 branches yes / 2 branches no]:**
 **Does not pass** on tie. No single branch breaks 2–2 alone.

 **MAJORITY-OF-MAJORITIES PROCESS [default unlock]:**
 [1] Each of **four branches** takes an **internal majority vote** [published
 ledger row per branch].
 [2] **Pass** requires **≥3 branch majorities** aligned [majority of
 majorities -- not 2–2].
 [3] If still **2–2** after one structured **conference round** [each branch
 sends bounded mandate; max **30 days**], **fail closed** -- status quo holds
 until new **legislature proposal** or **membrane** rehearing on **process
 defect** only [not merits re-vote by mob].

 **Ordinary issues:** single owning branch decides per jurisdiction table; no
 four-branch roll-call.

 **Picker blame [#6]:** bad membrane doctrine or bad law-court doctrine →
 appeal + **pickers who seated panel** answer at **their** election;
 judges stay until term/age unless **crime** removal.

 REJECT [operative]:
 - **Life tenure** or **death-seat** appointment lottery.
 - **One court** eating abortion + money + elections + agencies [incumbent SCOTUS
 cosplay -- rhymes SCOTUS NEUTRALITY THEATER].
 - **Judicial recall on merits** [populist veto on law cage].
 - **Fifth branch**, nested appeals across all four for same political question.

 FALSIFICATION [analyst revises if]: Age + term caps **without** jurisdiction
 split still produce **one-lane** capture [all valves move together]; or
 four-lane split **cannot** decide election/contributor disputes within
 **one cycle** [process default fails MI bar].

 PATHOS [operative -- analyst prior]:

 LOOKS-LIKE-PERSON LINE [law follows pathos]:
 | Phase | Pathos + law |
 |-------|----------------|
 | **Before 15 w gestational** [pre pin; ~10–12 nubs → ~16–20 baby band mid] | May end pregnancy **lawfully** [cells / pre-person lane]. |
 | **At or after 15 w** [**looks like person**] | **Child murder** -- serious-crime / death-tier; **zero** elective. |
 | **At or after 15 w -- medical NEEDED** | **Narrow exception only:** mother's **life or death** in **severe**
 documented crisis [two-physician or ledger equivalent; **not** mental-health
 stretch, **not** "convenience needed"]. Pathos still hates it; law admits
 **possible** true necessity -- blocks stupid "zero exception" bad-faith only. |
 | **Never** | Slow drift; convenience; post-line kill without **needed** life/death row. |

 **Rape [pre-line only]:**
 **Fast or don't** -- **before 15 w** only; separate statute row.

 **Medical [two lanes]:**
 **Before 15 w:** ectopic, crisis, mother risk -- **do it fast or don't**
 [no delay into person-shaped weeks].
 **At or after 15 w:** **only if NEEDED** -- **life-or-death of mother**
 **possible** without termination [severe physical; documented]. **Not**
 anxiety, depression, or social "health." **Not** rape relabel past line.
 Statute word **NEEDED** = high bar + audit [stupid-comment foil, not second
 elective lane].

 applies].

 same speakers call unborn **"not science"** [clump, parasite, non-person]

 enemy-definition care].

 **Pinned default [analyst 2026-10-10]:** **15 weeks gestational age**
 [from LMP -- standard clinical count]. Rationale: public **looks-like-
 person** bands center **~11 w** [10–12 head/limb nubs] and **~18 w**
 [16–20 clear baby shape] -- **midpoint ≈ 15 w**. **At or after 15 w:**
 **child murder** row [no elective]. **Before 15 w:** cells / pre-person-
 shaped lane only. Statistician may shift **±1 week** on instrument audit
 [**90% rule**]; **compact change** if moving default [four-branch critical
 class if applicable]. **Rape:** **before** line; **fast or don't**.
 **Medical after line:** **NEEDED** life/death-of-mother **only** [severe,
 documented -- see table].

 **NOTICE DUTY [base case -- 99% row]:**
 Sex with pregnancy risk → **missed period → test** [cheap, common]. **15 w**
 gives months for typical cycles. **"Didn't know until 20 w"** with capacity

 cycle disorder] → **named narrow lanes** [rape fast early, NEEDED medical,
 documented edge] -- **1% does not make 99% wrong**.

UNIFIED FEDERAL PERSON IDENTITY [analyst policy -- chat 2026-10-27]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst analyst policy -- not framework finding.
 Operative **merge-all-IDs** posture -- civil, benefits, travel, franchise
 on **one** person stack. Fictional policy reality [rhyme MANDATORY NET-
 SCORE].

 ONE-LINE:
 **One person, one merged ID; public number starts the process; use needs
 private proof.**

 MERGE [no parallel SKU forest]:
 **Retire** separate products as authoritative identity [voting-only card,
 benefits-only card, state DMV photo as unrelated numbering scheme, SSN
 **as** the thing you show]. **One** federal person record + **one**
 issuance / renewal path; states **admin inside** federal guidelines [forms,
 sites, hours -- rhyme POLL ID **admin meter**].

 PUBLIC NUMBER [fine to be public]:
 Person-key / registry number assigned at **birth or naturalization** --
 **starts** identity only. **May appear in public** [roll slices, mail,
 receipts] without equaling **use**. Number alone = **pointless for action**
 [lookup handle, not proof]. Knowing someone's number ≠ being them.

 PRIVATE LAYER [required to **use**]:
 **Acting as** that person requires **extra** proof per context -- pinned
 examples: physical token, biometric, PIN, live clerk check, pollbook
 strike ceremony. **Split:** public number = **who row**; private bundle =
 **may operate as row** [vote, travel gate, benefits desk, tax sign].

 FRANCHISE / VOTING:
 **Who row** links Roll B; **may mark** = ledger + civic pass; **poll use**
 = private layer + strike + ballot [MANDATORY NET-SCORE **IDENTITY AND
 PUBLIC NUMBERS**]. Five-check **[2] identity proved** = **use** layer passed,
 not number disclosed [CONTRIBUTOR LEDGER].

 Cross-ref: POLL ID IN-PERSON FRANCHISE ADMIN; CONTRIBUTOR LEDGER [five-check];
 MANDATORY NET-SCORE ELECTIONS; ELECTION MEASUREMENT SUBSTRATE [above].

MANDATORY NET-SCORE ELECTIONS [analyst policy -- chat 2026-10-27]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst analyst policy -- not framework
 finding. Operative ballot arithmetic for named offices. **Fictional policy
 reality:** corruption is not the default plot; rules encode **mandate
 legibility**, not assume routine vote-fixing or bigger-gov bypass.

 ONE-LINE:
 **Mandatory yes/no per candidate; net = yes − no; net ≤ 0 cannot win;
 public precinct math, secret ballot; holdover until net-positive winner.**

 SCOPE [mandatory -- no skip on filed names in race]:
 **State district seats**; **federal and state central offices**. Filing +
 party size caps keep ballots short. Franchise gate unchanged [CONTRIBUTOR
 LEDGER -- who may mark]. **Roll B duty to vote** [POLICY BULLETS] --
 abstention not allowed on called elections.

 BALLOT AND TALLY:
 Each voter marks **yes** [+1] or **no** [−1] on **every filed candidate**
 in a scoped race. **No abstain** on those lines.
 **Precinct count same night** -- no transfer rounds, no post-hoc formula
 as authoritative result. Publish per candidate: **yes**, **no**, **net**.
 **Secret ballot** -- no public voter-name ledger.

 WIN AND MULTI-SEAT:
 **Net > 0** required to take a seat. **Single seat:** highest **net**
 among qualifiers. **Multi-seat:** top **k** by **net** among rows with
 **net > 0** only; fewer than **k** qualifiers → vacant seats per statute.
 **Net tie at top** → **one** structured debate → **one** fast revote
 [rhyme Election tie [POLICY BULLETS]].

 ALL-NONPOSITIVE FIELD:
 **Every candidate net ≤ 0** = **valid result** [field rejection signal] --
 not counting failure. **No** seating by highest negative.

 NO QUALIFYING WINNER / RERUN:
 **Incumbent and lawful officeholders** remain for **routine government**
 and **next election administration** until a **net-positive winner** is
 seated. **Negative net on the ballot** does not block that interim
 holdover [caretaker by vacancy rule -- awkward accepted].
 **Rerun** on statute clock. Candidates **net ≤ 0** in that round **barred
 one cycle** from filing for **that office**.

 IDENTITY AND PUBLIC NUMBERS [no double vote]:
 Full merge + public-number / private-use split: **UNIFIED FEDERAL PERSON
 IDENTITY** [above]. **Poll stack:** private **use** proof → pollbook
 **strike** on person-key → **one** ballot → **in box**. **One person-key →
 one ballot** per election [master roll; no second strike]. Roll B =
 **may mark** [ledger + civic pass] -- separate from **who row**.
 **Public per precinct [same night, no names]:**
 **E** = eligible on **this precinct slice** of Roll B;
 **I** = ballots **issued** [strikes];
 **B** = ballots **in box** [counted pile].
 **I ≤ E**; **B = I**. Per candidate **j**: **Yes_j + No_j = I** [mandatory
 complete ballot -- each voter yes **or** no on every line].
 Publish **E / I / B** + **yes / no / net** per candidate. Mismatch =
 contest trigger [rhyme ELECTION MEASUREMENT SUBSTRATE]. **How** you voted
 stays secret; **that** arithmetic held is public.

 DISTINCT CLASS:
 **War mandate / constitutional compact** may retain **yes > 50% of total
 franchise roll** unless compact pins net-score for that question.

 Cross-ref: UNIFIED FEDERAL PERSON IDENTITY [above]; ELECTION MEASUREMENT
 SUBSTRATE [this block]; CONTRIBUTOR LEDGER AND FRANCHISE [Roll B]; POLL ID
 IN-PERSON FRANCHISE ADMIN; RECALL THRESHOLD TABLE [below]; POLICY BULLETS
 Election tie + Electoral design rows.

 RECALL THRESHOLD TABLE [load-bearing]:
 **30%** = start [petition / initiate].
 **50%** = normal removal pass.
 **55%** = very difficult removal pass.
 **40%** = minimum turnout [people → local ballot only].

 RECALL CASCADE:
 **DENOMINATOR RULE [load-bearing -- analyst 2026-11-05]:** **every threshold in this table is
 computed on Roll B, not population and not a registration list.** Roll B is the set with a
 **proof chain per person** [identity + earned lawful positive + civic pass]. **Population is
 not a denominator** -- it counts people who may not mark, so it is **unmeasurable**. **Existing
 registration rolls prove nothing.** Where a row below says "population," read **Roll B of that
 unit**.
 **PEOPLE → LOCAL** [normal 50%]:
 Start: **30%** of **Roll B** residents [90-day window; named official +
 numbered grounds + evidence packet].
 Ballot: 45–60 days after qualify; **≥50%** yes **and** **≥40%**
 turnout of all **Roll B**.
 Grounds: membrane failure; substrate block; Correction Theatre
 charter violation; refusal to enforce valid minimum [not party /
 vibes / unpopular single vote].
 Cooldown: failed petition 6 months; failed ballot 12 months same
 grounds; one live petition per official per term.
 **LOCAL → STATE** [normal 50%]:
 Start: local gov units covering **≥30%** of state **Roll B**.
 Confirm: **≥50%** of initiating locals [**Roll B-weighted** -- each local's share = its
 Roll B ÷ state Roll B] within 30 days.
 Pass: **≥50%** of state **Roll B** under **yes** locals.
 **ROLL B WEIGHTING -- STATED EFFECT [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** weighting by Roll B means
 a local with **large population but small Roll B** carries **less weight** in confirmations
 and in the pass threshold. **This is the rule working, not a defect** -- Roll B is the
 **only** population with a proof chain, so it is the only population entitled to weight.
 **But the effect has a direction that must be visible:**
 - A **poor local** has a **small Roll B** because many residents are net-negative on
 means-tested lines [the franchise rule] -- so **poor locals are structurally lighter in
 recall arithmetic** [rhyme POOR IMMOBILITY: this is the same asymmetry, showing up in a
 second place].
 - **Accepted, not hidden:** the fix for a small Roll B is **more residents earning lawful
 positives**, not a population weight that reintroduces unproven denominators.
 **Visibility pins [so the effect cannot be silent]:**
 **[v1]** Every local's **Roll B size and Roll B share of state** is **published** with the
 recall arithmetic [rhyme PUBLICATION DUTY]. A recall that fails because a large-population
 local was light must **show the weights that did it**.
 **[v2]** **Floor access panels remain the poor's own scoreboard** [WELFARE; RESOURCE PANEL] --
 a policy that shrinks Roll B in poor areas **shows up there** first, on the floor rows,
 **not only** as a quieter recall.
 **[v3]** A **local whose Roll B shrinks while its floor rows hold** [people are fed, housed,
 and still not qualifying] is a **franchise-design finding**, not a local failure -- it
 means the meter, not the place, needs review.
 **STATE → UNION** [very difficult 55%]:
 Start: **≥30%** of states [≥15 of 50] name commissioner or power.
 Confirm: **≥50%** of initiating states within 60 days.
 Vote: **≥2/3** of all states within 90 days.
 Pass: **≥55%** of states **yes**.

 **BLOCK-AND-ESCALATE RUNG [analyst 2026-11-06 -- tier design borrowed from ABSTRACT New America
 form; thresholds stay ours]:** thresholds and blocking are **different axes** -- **30 / 50 / 55** say
 **how many sign**; a **block rung** says **who may stop whom**. Bolt them together, do not swap:
 · **Open case at the low tier.** Local supermajority at **30% start** initiates removal for an
 openly stupid / evil official [the **cheap-to-prove** case -- openness is legible, so it does not
 need the difficult bar].
 · **State may block -- on record, with a stated reason.** A block is **legitimate** [state is a real
 tier, not a rubber stamp], but an **unreasoned block is a stall, not a block** ⇒ auto-escalate
 [prevents the state rung from becoming landlord capture].
 · **Double block → central takes it and must decide.** Not sit. This is the
 anti-passive-dysfunction rung -- the failure mode is a center that lets the block stand
 [rhyme **PASSIVE DYSFUNCTION**; **CENTRAL FLOOR SUBSTRATE** -- central's job is arbitration on a
 frozen stack, not routine governance].
 · **Sneaky case at the 55% tier.** Structurally captured but not openly bad goes to 55% very
 difficult -- exactly the case a removal ladder cannot see [language kept, machinery hollowed; rhyme
 theory **LEGAL PREFIX WITHOUT LOGOS BENCH**].
 ⇒ **two-case split, held:** **open evil = 30% internal mechanism**; **sneaky evil = 55% late
 mechanism**. Rhymes ABSTRACT **sneaky-evil trim lane** [**NEW AMERICA CONSTITUTIONAL REPUBLIC
 GOVERNANCE**] -- internal ladder handles the visible case, the hard tier handles the invisible one.
 **Not imported:** New America's exact rungs or three-person central shape [that is their form];
 **only the tier logic is borrowed**.

 NON-RECALL EXITS:
 Resign → special election 60–90 days.
 Incapacity → separate panel bar [not 30/50/55].
 Law-break [any office] → remove + prosecute.

 HOSTILE PATH WITHIN INCUMBENT US [orientation -- chat 2026-07-26

 → **state cell** [AG, legislature, grant refusal, non-cooperation,

 HINGE COLLAPSE [orientation -- chat 2026-07-26; not framework finding]:

 JUDICIAL [separate species]:
 **10 years** maximum at any court level. **Non-renewable.** **Age 55
 immediate vacate** [same as elected offices]. **No recall on merits.**
 Ruling quality within law is **not** removal -- no "stupid ruling"
 ground. Crime or law-break [bribery, fabrication, procedure denial] →
 panel remove + prosecute immediately [independent prosecutor clock].
 Unpopular lawful ruling → **appeal** in correct **branch court** only
 [jurisdiction table in OFFICE TENURE slice]; judge serves until **10-year**
 term or age **55**, whichever first; **pickers** own seating blame at
 **their** next election.
 Local judges elected; higher courts = **panel confirm + sortition slice**
 [no single-movement appointment pipe]. STV / multi-member if elected --
 no safe judicial district. Full stack: OFFICE TENURE AND BRANCH
 JURISDICTION [POLICY SLICES].

 but ledger red for years; rescue exceeds sunset; center taxes persons
 for general ops → architecture failed at cage layer.

 Hostile-path ladder stalls: state capture + compacts + non-cooperation

NATION CULTURE AND TRIBAL BAIT [analyst policy -- no social content]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Frame: every nation should carry a **primary ethnic state and culture**
 [host people + shared customs + public language + law-shaped norms].
 Pluralism OK as **seasoning**; assimilation toward host fabric is the
 deal. **Diversity-as-ideology** [no core, no assimilation, no
 consequences for antisocial public behavior] = chaos line -- reject when
 shared order breaks [public transit, shared enclosed space, basic
 decency floor].
 **MEMBERSHIP≠CULT FOIL [analyst pin 2026-10-18 -- load-bearing]:**
 **A nation is a membership institution** -- bounded **we**, host fabric,
 law-shaped norms, public obligations [assimilation deal above; AEC **covenant
 republic** leg]. Objection *"don't make polity feel like a membership cult"*
 = **freedom-as-no-we** [venue + rights talk + rotating jerseys, not a polity].
 **Affirm:** membership + charter rows [CONTRACT BAND EXPORT §1 compact contract
 floor; truth-primary; **simple public ask → yes/no or numbered honest limit**
 on accountability -- not hour-long evasion]. **Reject [actual cult SKU]:** closed
 epistemic capture, guru/faction owns all valves, **no priced exit**, extract
 sacrament without ledger -- rhyme **DEME / disorder**, not nation. **Reject
 [faction cartel misread]:** nation/contract-band telos ≠ one jersey owns everything;
 **structure not tribe** [AEC OPERATIVE DOCTRINE #2] = ratification + published
 ledger + recall teeth, not enemy-cohesion cosplay. **One-line:** calling bounded
 demos a "cult" avoids naming **who the polity is for**.
 **REGISTER MISMATCH specimen:** revolutionary public
 register + host-nation assimilation in private life scores mechanism not moral
 verdict on marriage/parenting; [CASE:ASMONGOLD] #27; assimilation toward host
 fabric remains the deal above.

CELL NATION PERSONAL POLICY [analyst policy -- chat 2026-07-10]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst analyst policy -- not framework
 finding. No social content [not Section Nine]. Cell/virus metaphor =
 structural read on polity form, not biology claim or purge license.
 Cross-ref: NATION CULTURE AND TRIBAL BAIT; POLICY POSITIONS nation band
 POLITICAL VS PERSONAL BOUNDARY [theory ANALYST NOTES].

 FRAME [cell vs virus]:
 **Cell** = generative polity: nests, reciprocates, continues place +
 people + duty. **Virus** = devolved-fast replicator: hijacks host,
 sheds nesting, needs fresh hosts. Big should be **cell form**, not
 viral form at scale. CA read = membrane/export node -- success as
 strain, failure as polity on cell scoreboard.
 Virus-shaped ≠ always harmful; at scale = **step 1 medium** →
 **step 2 harm** [extractors, platforms, rent, burnout]. Nation
 should not optimize for step-1 attractor.

 POLICY STACK [four layers]:
 **NATION** -- membrane, defaults, tax skew, institutions, anti-exploit.
 **PERSONAL** -- adult private life: choice, conscience, association
 protected; not the national standard.
 **HOUSEHOLD** -- personal meets duty when dependents exist.
 **CHILD** -- protected development; nation overrides personal
 conflict.

 NATION LOGIC [operative]:
 [1] Standard for raising kids ≠ standard for adult life. Adults wide
 liberty; child layer stricter [stability, bounded sexuality, duty].
 [2] Stability and duty, not identity labels: skepticism at **churn,
 adult drama in kid space, access+grooming** -- not bingo-card
 classes. Abuse policy = access + power + reporting; not "label =
 risk" [not supported for gay men / class guilt].
 [3] Thick adult–minor membrane: no adult sexuality in child
 institutions; mandatory reporting; two-adult rules where possible
 minors out of adult dating/sex/identity economies.
 [4] No identity projects on children: age-appropriate bounds; no
 institutional secrecy vs parents for minors; no irreversible
 medical path before adulthood except narrow clinical bar; adult
 trans life post-18 = personal layer.
 [5] Incent nudge, don't force: single = normal tax baseline; couple +
 dependent children = modest relief; no marriage mandate, no birth
 mandate; honor keepers in zoning/prestige without punishing
 childless.
 [6] Skeptical of novelty where kids are cargo: new adult models =
 adult choice until generational stability proven; institutions
 serve child standard, not membrane export standard.
 **Sequencing :** nation funds subsistence floor +
 membrane before culture/contact reform hours consume the week.
 [7] Adoption [identity-neutral child bar]: same caregiver standard for
 all households -- stability, low churn, access+grooming rules; bar =
 median+ quality or higher on behavioral record. Gay couple adopt
 permitted; no favorable or hostile identity-specific adoption
 statutes [neither equity carve-out nor ban jersey].

 PERSONAL LAYER [operative]:
 [1] Personal sovereignty default: state enters only for crime, fraud,
 abuse, neglect, coercion -- not non-standard lifestyle, atheism,
 singlehood, adult trans life after 18.
 [2] Personal ≠ no duty: contracts, dependents fed, no neighbor harm,
 no grooming -- still nation job. Free adult, not extractive adult.
 [3] Personal stops at child door: parent rights persist; child
 standard wins inside household and institutions.
 [4] Personal not standard: "your path is yours" + "public standard
 is nesting, stability, continuity."
 [5] Personal reciprocity [soft]: show up for block/work/place; don't
 treat town as motel; don't evangelize devolved-fast as only modern
 life. Tolerance, not centrality.
 [6] Personal tax baseline: single adult = 1.0× reference; household +
 dependents = modest credit/threshold bump; cap credits; no singles
 tax, no marriage police.

 WANT vs TOLERATE:
 Tolerate [adults]: single, childless, mobile, atheist, believer,
 non-nesting life, adult transition after 18.
 Want [polity + children]: settled caregivers, local continuity, thick
 child membrane, nesting cheaper than membrane life, extractors
 constrained, immigration favors settlement/joiners over infinite
 churn.

 TRANS + CHILDREN [same logic, no purge]:
 Adults: live as you choose; nation doesn't hunt.
 Minors: no automatic affirmation pipeline; no clinic bypassing parents
 no school secrecy on identity/body; counseling first; irreversible
 medicine after adulthood except rare overseen harm-prevention; sex-
 based bounds for minors where bodies/safety matter.

 CRIMINAL LINE:
 Prosecute hijack with victims: fraud, theft, trafficking, abandonment,
 predatory design on minors, coercive control.
 Do not criminalize virus-**shape** without harm: childless, gay, atheist,
 single, porn user, mover -- not crimes.
 Nation dries medium [housing, tax, platform duty, settlement] -- does
 not jail non-nesting peaceful adults.

 ONE-LINE:
 Nation sets the cell; personal holds adult choice; household activates
 duty; child wins on conflict -- single taxed normal, couple-with-child
 lightly favored, personal never standard but always real; critical of
 adult experiments on kids and extractive form at scale, zero forced
 marriage or childbirth.

 T3 Medium: cell/virus metaphor operative for analyst; margin band and
 immigration settlement detail open. Merge candidate: applied POLICY
 POSITIONS nation band when promoted.

FAIR LAW AND PRIVATE LITIGATION [analyst policy -- chat 2026-07-11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 FAIR LAW [criminal + civil procedure -- analyst policy v25
 chat 2026-07-11]:
 Core: law is not a priced boundary until guilt or liability
 merits. Until then, cost is public substrate -- not routed onto
 accused, poor plaintiff, or anonymous critic.
 NO COST UNTIL END + GUILT:
 Criminal: zero court/filing/surcharge fees on defendant until
 conviction; state-funded defense at parity with prosecution; bail
 = secured appearance bond, fully returnable; pretrial detention
 only on documented flight/violence risk with fast review.
 Civil: each side bears own costs until final judgment; loser-pays
 only after liability finding -- not at filing, discovery, or
 interim motion.
 Anti-priced-routing: no pre-merits identity discovery, ministerial
 subpoenas, or choke-by-retainer paths; state/corp may not impose
 career or platform penalties before conviction or civil judgment
 [fire-before-trial block].
 LONGER TRIAL -> MORE COST [on delayer]:
 Statutory clocks [example bands]: misdemeanor 90 days; felony
 180 days; civil 12 months to merits trial unless complex-case
 order with written findings.
 Each day past clock -> automatic cost shift to delaying party
 [frivolous continuance, discovery abuse, prosecution sandbagging].
 Court backlog past clock -> court budget bears cost, not parties.
 Discovery cap: fixed exchange windows; sanctions = cost shift +
 strike, not new priced gates.
 REAL AND FAST TRIALS ONLY:
 Trial required for any liberty loss > 30 days unless defendant
 waives in writing after counsel explains cost-free trial right.
 No guilty plea cheaper than trial by fee, bail, or surcharge.
 Bench trial on request; jury on request for liberty cases.

 **LAWFARE WASTE LEDGER AND MEGA-DUMP QUANT [analyst chat 2026-10-19 --
 FAIR LAW sibling; extends CORP LAWFARE COST; v7.3 quant sketch]:**
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Design preference -- not framework finding.
 **Winning = mostly burning money [operative read]:** at megacorp scale,
 partial merits "win" often **net loss** -- fees, engineering lock, customer
 deal stall, and calendar harm **>>** jury award [2026 mega-corp license
 siege: split verdict + **nominal** damages = headline liability, not
 reimbursement]. Policy must **recapture waste imposed**, not trophy dollars.
 **Waste ledger [mandatory per matter]:** running account of **public**
 court cost + **private** defense/compliance cost **attributable to
 sanctionable segments** [bifurcate when merits mixed -- not whole-case
 tax on good-faith claims].
 **Quant [not fixed nominal]:** on logos-effort / abuse finding, award =
 ledger × multiplier band -- e.g. **100% public + 200-500% private** on
 negligent waste; higher for **intentional** interference or **discovery/
 motion choke**; floor so corporate burn cannot round to zero.
 **Escalation ladder [repeat pattern]:** [1] first finding -- cost shift +
 multiplier on slice; [2] second in ~36mo [any court] -- mandatory **bond**
 for next high-stakes filing + higher mult; [3] third -- **deterrence band**
 [% of dispute legal spend or entity litigation budget -- scales past
 fee-insensitive actors]; [4] fraud-on-court / documented repeat after
 warning -- **nuke band** [below]. Registry of findings -- not one-off
 loss = innocence.
 **Nuke band [structured discretion only]:** apex may impose **up to cap**
 [cap = **ratio to the measured waste ledger** on the matter, **or** a **published
 multiple of the entity's prior annual legal spend**, **whichever is lower** -- **no
 fixed dollar figure in statute**; a hard number goes stale and invites the
 fee-insensitive actor to price it in] **only**
 after written findings + separate remedy hearing [clear-and-convincing or
 statutory equivalent] + appeal as of right. **Reject:** unstructured
 judge picks any billion on whim [vagueness + capture]. Purpose classes:
 restitution [ledger], deterrence [budget/revenue %], not random punishment.
 **Symmetric:** plaintiff **and** defendant -- defense mega-dump when corp
 or state burns clock without logos effort [pairs dual mega-dump above].
 **Early gate:** plausibility / anti-SLAPP-class kill inside ~60-90d --
 **full ledger from day one** on dismissed abusive track [stops discovery
 as punishment before merits].
 **Tradeoff [pairs FIVE FUNDAMENTALS proportionality]:** accept **less
 cheap court access** for repeat strategic burners in exchange for **not
 subsidizing siege** -- deterrence over maximal filing convenience at scale.
 Cross-ref: CORP LAWFARE COST; FAIR LAW [LONGER TRIAL clocks]; PRIVATE
 CIVIL LITIGATION [SLAPP]; PROPORTIONALITY [FIVE FUNDAMENTALS #5].

 CRIMINAL STATUTE AND PROSECUTION NON-TWIST [analyst policy
 chat 2026-07-23]:
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst criminal-law design preference --
 not framework finding. Pairs FAIR LAW [process] with **substance +
 discretion** limits.
 Core: **no twisting; no big vague holes.** If conduct must be
 criminal, **write it narrowly and add it explicitly** -- do not
 stretch old statutes to fit today's target.
 BARGAIN [false-negative preferred]:
 **Two guilty slip = fine.** Better than **one hundred** innocent,
 marginal, or selectively charged people **twisted** through elastic
 law + directional discretion. Rule-of-law over maximum capture.
 TWISTING [both directions -- same hole]:
 **Hard twist:** stack charges, terrorism label, public pre-conviction
 branding, grand-jury shopping, pretrial detention without
 documented flight/violence risk.
 **Soft twist:** decline, undercharge, lenient plea bargain, slow-walk close,
 immunize associates -- when connected or politically useful.
 Documented rhyme buckets [chat 2026-07-23]: elite **decline/NPA**
 [Epstein-class]; mass-event **tiered leniency** [J6 misdemeanor
 pleas, dropped obstruction after Fischer] vs **aggressive stack**
 on disfavored protesters [Broadview-class material-support push].
 Twist runs through **legal holes**, not naked fiction.
 VAGUE HOLES [close or narrow]:
 Material support without group or specific planned crime; terrorism
 sentencing on thin "influence government" read; true threat from
 manifesto tone + disclaimer as concealment ["in Minecraft" =
 coded-speech hole, not shield]; conspiracy on loose agreement
 declination/NPA with no victim notice and unnamed immunized co-
 conspirators; informant stings with weak predication.
 DESIGN RULES [operative]:
 [1] **Elements explicit** -- charge the act proved, not the person
 or ideology.
 [2] **New conduct → new statute** -- debated, voted, public; no
 shadow lawmaking via charging theory or agency rule games.
 [3] **Same text for everyone** -- selective declination and selective
 aggressive prosecution both violate; soft and hard twist both violate.
 [4] **Discretion logged** -- declination, enhancement, charge
 downgrade: recorded reason; grand-jury misconduct not redacted
 from court.
 [5] **Terrorism / material-support labels** only when statutory
 elements charged and proved -- not context garnish.
 ONE-LINE:
 Tight law, explicit updates, tolerate some slippage -- vague law
 does not catch more offenders; it gives enforcers a dial.
 T3 Medium: analyst preference; implementation bands [statutory
 drafting, prosecutorial guidelines, judicial review] open.

 [fire-before-trial]; POLICY BULLETS Crime; COALITION ENFORCEMENT DUAL
 MEMBRANE [operative read]; FIREARMS [ghost-gun rule clarity vs stretch].

 PRIVATE CIVIL LITIGATION [narrow allow -- analyst policy v25
 chat 2026-07-11]:
 Default: dislike. Most private civil litigation today = priced
 routing [wealth, procedure, discovery as weapon; merits second].
 Prefer public prosecution + public defense + fast merits trial.
 Allow private suit only when all hold:
 [1] Named plaintiff suffered documented substrate harm.
 [2] No speech-routing, anonymity-stripping, or pre-merits
 fishing [lawfare / SLAPP class].
 [3] Public cost until liability found; loser-pays after
 judgment only [FAIR LAW block].
 [4] Statutory speed clock; delayer pays past deadline.
 [5] Remedy cap: restitution and correction -- no punitive or
 fee multiples beyond proportionality.
 Good instances [overflow valve when state path weak]:
 identifiable victim + identifiable harm [bodily injury, fraud,
 measurable loss]; contract between voluntary equals after exit
 failed; cost externalization back to deployer [opioid Tier 3
 pattern]; executive personal liability backup when state
 prosecution stalls [rhymes nuclear liability policy].
 Bad instances [reject]:
 defamation/reputation/IP as silence router; wealthy plaintiff vs
 uncounseled defendant; class-action counsel-capture theater
 substitute for state duty on crime, monopoly, corruption, public
 safety; **pre-dispute adhesion arbitration / class waiver** [ADHESION
 CONSENT REJECT -- state forum mandatory person vs corp].
 **DEFAMATION VS THE MARK [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** the rejection above is of
 **defamation as a silenced-router / wealth-weapon** -- **not** a claim that reputation
 is never remediable. **Split by who decides and what is proven:**
 - **Nation proving and marking a dog-whistle** [violence bait, plausibly deniable
 incitement, extractor dog-whistle] = **MARK, not defamation** [MARK VS REMEDY].
 The nation publishes **what the record shows** on a **due-process finding**
 [notice, hearing, appeal -- VIOLENCE BAIT statute shape]. **Not useful as a
 defamation action** and **not routed through private litigation at all** --
 it is a **public finding**, which is why it does not need the defamation lane.
 - **Private person suing over a false factual claim that destroyed livelihood** =
 **harm lane** [HARM VS FEELING half 2: livelihood damage is harm], with the
 **asymmetric-cost guard intact**: **no** wealthy plaintiff vs uncounseled
 defendant, **no** IP/reputation as silence router, **no** suit over **opinion,
 insult, or described reality** [feeling lane].
 **The distinction that makes it work:** **a mark is the state saying what it found; a
 defamation suit is a party asking the state to punish speech.** Mark = **public
 record**. Suit = **private remedy for proven falsehood with proven damage**. **Dog-
 whistle marking never needs the second** -- the state is not claiming damages, it is
 **naming a pattern on the record** [rhyme VIOLENCE BAIT: "plausibly deniable whistle
 still aimed at blood" is **markable** without being **actionable**].

 **TRUTH IS NEVER DEFAMATION [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05; closes the residual]:** **a true
 statement cannot be defamatory.** *"You are a pedo"* said of someone **who is one** is
 **not defamation** -- it is **a true statement about a fact**. **Truth is a complete
 defense, and the only question is whether it is true.**
 **What this closes:** the earlier residual [livelihood destroyed by a **true** statement] is
 **not a defamation lane and never was** -- it is **a fact being known**. **A person harmed
 by a true statement is harmed by the truth, not by the speaker.** **The state does not
 compensate for accurate information** [rhyme HARM VS FEELING: harm requires an act, and
 speaking a true fact is not a wrong act].
 **What remains actionable [and only this]:** **a false statement of fact** that **damaged
 livelihood** [above]. **Not** true statements, **not** opinion, **not** insult, **not**
 described reality, **not** a true statement delivered cruelly [cruelty may take the
 **mark** lane -- VIOLENCE BAIT / ASYMMETRIC ACCUSATION -- never the damages lane].
 **Burden and shape, stated plainly:** **the person claiming defamation must prove the
 statement FALSE** -- not that it hurt, not that it was unfair, not that it was unkind.
 **Falsity is the element.** If they cannot prove it false, **the statement stands** and the
 remedy fails [rhyme REMEDY IS EARNED BY PROOF: no proof, no payout].
 **Why this protects the stack's own function:** most of what this file exists to name
 [extractor patterns, organized predation, dog-whistles, elite capture] is **true and
 damaging to the named party**. A defamation lane that punishes **damaging truths** would
 delete the file's own diagnostic apparatus. **Truth stays a defense or the kit cannot
 speak.**
 **Void / fail:** a defamation award where the statement was **substantially true** =
 failed. A ruling that weighed **distress** against **truth claims** and preferred distress
 = pathos-as-fact = failed [PATHOS CANNOT SUBSTITUTE FOR FACT]. Truth treated as **one
 factor among many** rather than **dispositive** = failed.

EEO SELF-ID AND EMPLOYMENT PROXY FILTER [analyst policy -- analyst chat
 2026-10-16; retail applicant EEO UX + enforcement gap]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst operative read on U.S. employment
 voluntary race/ethnicity collection -- not framework finding, not legal
 advice. Distinct from franchise CULTURE FILTER [behavior routing] and
 from slur-primary discrimination doctrine.

 ONE-LINE:
 **Applicant race self-ID primarily feeds aggregate compliance and
 reputation metrics**; **title-VII bar on race-based hire does not stop
 maintained proxy filters** [class, credit, record, geography, culture-fit]
 that **statically skew** by race. **Compliance theater** when anti-bias
 copy sits beside **forced or non-declinable** EEO fields and **proxy
 sort** still runs.

 LAYERS [operative -- not employer intent verdict]:
 [1] **Federal bucket reporting** [EEO-1 / applicant-flow] -- institutional
 race/ethnicity tagging for government and audit files; not an applicant
 morality or anti-racism test.
 [2] **Adverse-impact read** -- only layer with clear anti-bias job:
 selection procedure vs outcome skew; useless if numbers filed and filters
 unchanged.
 [3] **Public inclusion metrics** -- workforce and board diversity
 percentages, pay-equity tables by race; same buckets, reputational and
 investor-facing layer atop [1].
 [4] **Voluntary posture vs UX** -- EEOC long position: race/sex/ethnic
 disclosure **voluntary**; should be **separate from hire decision** and
 applicants **so advised**. **Blocking** online application until EEO
 questionnaire completes = **not lawful voluntary collection** [EEOC
 informal discussion letters on Internet hiring]. **No decline option**
 contradicts model invitation language [refusal must not adverse-treat;
 employer may impute via visual survey / other available information for
 reporting].
 [5] **Skip self-ID tactic** -- limited privacy: moves **when** label
 attaches, not whether pool is race-neutral. Interview and public signals
 often assign category anyway; **presents-as-white-male** commonly
 imputed white for reporting whether or not box checked.
 [6] **Proxy compression** -- "illegal to hire on race" does not idle
 sorting: **poor / risky / unstable / not leadership material** narratives
 routed through **neutral-coded gates** [income, credit, criminal history,
 gap, commute, school tier, culture-fit MSC] can **compress Black and poor
 applicants** without race on the form. Rhymes DELIBERATE FILTER
 DISCRIMINATION when **list + filter + maintained skew** holds -- EEO
 checkbox does not unwind proxy geometry.

 POLICY STACK [rhyme DELIBERATE FILTER -- employment lane]:
 [1] **Audit filters and pools**, not slogan charts or self-ID completion
 rates alone.
 [2] **Reject forced EEO self-ID** without decline and without **truly
 separate** storage from selection file [pair with FAIR LAW process read].
 [3] **Do not treat applicant self-ID** as proof org is non-discriminatory
 or as substitute for **disparate-impact** audit on real gates.
 [4] **Merit / apprentice path** when filters class-neutral [PAID
 APPRENTICE; ban culture-fit MSC per DELIBERATE FILTER stack].

NATION EXIT LANE [analyst policy -- chat 2026-11-05; fills the empty exit row -- §7]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst policy -- not framework finding. Supplies the
 **exit** leg that franchise, floor, referee, and charter rows all assume but that
 nothing in this file previously stated.
 **Home rule:** voice can be **gated**; exit **must not** be. A stack that gates voice
 **and** removes exit is a **trap**, not a polity [rhyme ABSTRACT §8: **demonic nations
 only survive by force + no exits**].

 ONE-LINE:
 **Any member may leave with their persons, their usage rights, and their earned
 hold, less a one-time 10% exit levy; the nation may not hold them by force, and
 leaving is one-way.**

 WHAT EXIT MEANS [load-bearing]:
 **[1] Voluntary leave** -- emigration is a **right**, not a privilege earned on the
 ledger. **Net-negative and muted members leave too** -- mute is a **voice gate**, never a
 **movement gate**. Any attempt to condition exit on franchise status = **lock-in** =
 demonic tell.
 **[2] Reason is helpful, not mandatory** -- stating why [routing, handoff, who still owes
 what] is **safety-positive**; withholding a reason is **not** automatic guilt. **Secret
 exit while hiding = breach-shaped** [reads as run or enemy op; no clean bar path]. Rhyme
 voluntary-exit read.
 **[3] What leaves with you:** persons, **built/structure usage rights on sale**
 [LAND HOUSING -- usage rights, not dirt title], and **earned hold above the hoard cap**
 [UNPRODUCTIVE HOARD CAP still binds at the cap]. **Not carried:** nation land, franchise,
 seat, or any claim on the floor of the nation you left.
 **[3b] EXIT LEVY [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** **one-time 10% levy on value taken
 out**, posted in advance, **paid once, at departure**. **Not** a recurring claim, **not**
 a wealth tax, **not** a gate [if you can pay it you can go]. **Purpose:** the leaver
 takes **stuff** -- and **stuff is most of the value** -- so the polity that **built the
 substrate** keeps a **tenth** and the leaver keeps **nine-tenths**. **Justification is
 thin and stated:** settlement, infrastructure, and floor investment are **real costs
 already borne**; the levy is a **settling of accounts**, not a penalty for leaving.
 **Poor cap [rhyme POOR IMMOBILITY block]:** the levy is **10% of what the leaver actually
 carries** -- **never a cash requirement**, and a leaver with **near nothing** owes
 **near nothing**. **10% of nothing is nothing**; a levy that **demands funds the leaver
 does not have** is a **poor-gate**, not a levy.
 **Design pins:** **flat 10%**, **no bracket by destination**, **no exemption for
 hostile destinations** [sovereignty cost of exit, **not** a foreign-policy lever],
 **published schedule**, **no negotiation at the window**. **Void:** levy that **rises**
 because you are leaving late, **scales** with destination, or is **waived** for approved
 destinations = **gate, not levy** = failed.
 **[4] Exit is one-way [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** **leave and never come back** is
 the **default**. Re-entry is **not** a right, **is not** implied by clean exit, and is
 **not** a "welcome back" lane -- a former member is an **immigrant** on the ordinary
 row [NATION MEMBRANE: skeptical, minimal, control + tracking]. **Why one-way:** a
 **round-trip** exit makes leaving **costless test-driving** -- leave in a bad year,
 return when the floor improves, with **no stake** in either. **One-way exit is what
 makes the choice mean something.**
 **[4b] Other nations are not this policy's problem [analyst]:** **admission elsewhere
 is their row, not ours.** The nation does **not** owe a leaver a **destination**, does
 **not** negotiate **receiving terms**, and does **not** condition its own exit rule on
 another polity's willingness to accept. **Stateless exit is a real outcome and an
 accepted one** -- exit is **permission to go**, not **guarantee of arrival**. **The
 nation carries the floor for people inside the membrane; it does not carry the world.**
 **[5] Leaving might not be easy [analyst -- honesty pin]:** exit being a **right** does
 not mean it is **cheap, quick, or painless**. **Real costs stay real:** selling usage
 rights, moving stuff, losing local network, losing the floor, arriving somewhere with no
 standing. **The policy promises: no one is *held*.** It does **not** promise **easy**.
 **This is the honest limit and it is load-bearing:** a polity that **sells** easy exit
 and delivers **hard** exit is **lying**; a polity that **names** hard exit and still
 refuses to **hold** people has done the thing.
 **[6] Return** -- see [4]: **one-way by default**. Clean exit does **not** guarantee
 re-admission.

 **Poor immobility is the capture surface [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05; load-bearing]:**
 **poor people always have it the worst, and nations want that.** Exit is an **asset-holder
 instrument** -- moving takes money, saleable rights, and a receiving place. **The poorest
 carry the least and lose the floor on the way out**, so **exit is weakest exactly where the
 mute bites hardest**. That is **not an accident of this design; it is the standing
 incentive of every polity** -- **immobility is the cheapest thing to tax**.
 **Therefore exit is NOT the poor's protection** [it is the rich's]. The poor's protection
 must be **inside** the membrane, and it is **four rows**:
 **[a] Floor cannot be conditioned** -- no work-for-floor, no fee-for-floor, no conduct
 requirement. A floor you can lose is **leverage**, and leverage on immobile people is
 **the extraction lane**.
 **[b] Mute is voice only, forever** -- a net-negative member keeps **floor, access, care,
 and movement**. Any coupling of franchise status to **provision** = **capture** = failed
 [this is the trap other polities run: *stay dependent or lose everything*].
 **[c] Exit is a poor's instrument** -- a net-negative member leaving pays a **share of what they
 have**, not a **liquidity test**. **Levy cannot exceed what they carry**; a levy that
 requires **finding cash** is a **gate built for the poor specifically**.
 **[d] Voice must be reachable without assets** -- **earned lawful positives** must include
 **wage labor, production, and small trade**, so a person with **no capital** can still
 clear Roll B. If the only positives are **dividends, rent, and land-use fees**, then
 **ownership is the franchise** and the mute becomes a **property test** -- which is
 **not this stack's rule** [LAWFUL EARNED POSITIVE: profit from work, not from being
 handed money].
 **The demonic read [testable]:** a nation that **profits from immobility** -- floor weak,
 exit hard, mute wide -- is **running the trap**, and it will **call that order**.
 **Falsification:** exit cost **higher for the poor than the rich** as a share of what
 they carry = **poor-gate** = failed. Floor **conditioned** on behavior or franchise =
 leverage = failed. Roll B clearable **only** through owned capital = **property test** =
 failed. Nation **gains revenue or compliance** from the fact that members **cannot
 leave** = **immobility rent** = capture read, not policy.

 COMPETITIVE READ [why exit is a feature]:
 A nation that must **trap** its people to keep them has already lost the argument.
 Nations **earn** stay [reform, delivery, honest ledger] or they **shrink**. Rhyme ABSTRACT
 **Contract-band scale vs mirror nations**: "nations cannot force outcomes on people who can leave";
 **lock-in = demonic by its own read**.

 WHAT EXIT FIXES / DOES NOT:
 **Fixes:** removes the "every error must be corrected through a narrow gate by the
 electorate that mutes the losers" failure; gives capture-suffering members with
 **assets** a **real alternative forum** [rhyme REFEREE LEGIBILITY STACK [d]]; makes the
 mute **bearable for the mobile** -- voice is gated, leaving is not, so a muted member is
 **silenced, not imprisoned**.
 **Does not fix:** the floor constituency problem [muted members still have no vote, only
 an alternative]; brain-drain cost of exit on the polity that stays; hostile-nation
 absorption of departees; **and it does not make leaving easy** -- exit is a **right**,
 not a **service** [pin [5]]. **The poorest have the hardest exit** [they carry least value
 out and lose the floor], so exit is **least useful to exactly the people the mute hurts
 most** -- **named, not hidden** [full read: POOR IMMOBILITY IS THE CAPTURE SURFACE below].
 That is the honest limit of this leg: it caps **imprisonment**, it does not cap
 **hardship**. **The poor's protection is the floor + the voice-only mute, not the exit.**

 FALSIFICATION:
 Exit conditioned on franchise status, tax clearance as a **fine**, or passport surrender
 = **lock-in** = failed [demonic read]. Exit paperwork not **posted and affordable** =
 priced-exit pin failed. A **muted** member blocked from leaving while their floor claim is
 voided = trap = failed. Nation **advertises** free exit while administratively denying it
 [denial rate unpublished] = measurement theater. **Exit levy above 10%, banded by
 destination, or negotiable at the window** = levy became **gate** = failed. **Round-trip
 return lane** [leave and come back freely] = **one-way pin failed** = exit became
 test-driving. **Exit conditioned on finding a receiving nation** = **destination
 guarantee** = failed [other nations are not this row].

OPEN PATHOS OR OPEN LOGOS / UNMARKED MIX DISCIPLINE [analyst chat 2026-10-10
-- bias-labeled; operative position]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst register discipline -- not framework finding.
 Rhymes applied ETHOS PATHOS LOGOS [pathos on object vs logos on use-chain];
 AMORAL HONESTY REGISTER VS MORAL COSTUME; SCOTUS NEUTRALITY THEATER / LOGOS
 COSPLAY; CLIMATE SIMPLE REGS HONESTY ASYMMETRY; [CASE:KIRSCHE] #22 [#C].

 ONE-LINE:
 **Respect open pathos or open logos; unmarked mixing is demonic-class routing
 or high-risk costume -- label the register before demanding action.**

 TWO CLEAN REGISTERS [do not sell as one substance]:
 **[1] Open pathos** -- grief, contact dread, institutional distrust, mercy-
 hunger or mercy-refusal **named as feeling**: "this is what I feel; falsify on
 facts or meter." Quiet pathos [no spectacle] and loud pathos [Foxu volume]
 differ in **ethics and audience capture**, not in whether pathos is declared.
 **[2] Open logos** -- statute, docket, obligation tags, classification rules
 **named as instrument read**: "this is what the record says; show the error."
 Admits revision when primary lands.

 **PATHOS CANNOT SUBSTITUTE FOR FACT [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05; hard rule]:** pathos is
 a **legitimate register** [register 1] and an **illegitimate proof**. **A feeling, however
 real, however strong, however widely shared, is never evidence that a factual claim is
 true.**
 **The rule in one line:** **pathos may argue that something matters; it may never argue
 that something is so.** *"This is devastating"* is a claim about **weight**. *"This is
 happening"* is a claim about **fact** and needs **the record** [rhyme HARM VS FEELING:
 feeling is real and chosen; harm is documented].
 **What pathos legitimately does:** sets **priority** [what to look at first], carries
 **weight** [how much it matters], supplies **motive** [why anyone should care], and marks
 **dread** [what a person fears will happen]. **All four are routing inputs, none are
 findings.**
 **What it may never do:** stand in for a **measurement**, a **docket**, a **vote count**,
 a **disparity figure**, a **causal claim**, or a **finding of guilt**. **A claim whose only
 support is intensity of feeling is an assertion with a costume on.**
 **Symmetry [load-bearing]:** this binds **both directions**. *"Think of the victims"* does
 not establish a fact about an incident; *"I'm just being realistic"* does not establish one
 either. **The register rule is about evidence, not about whose feelings are approved.**
 **Why it is load-bearing for the whole file:** every panel in this stack fires on a
 **published number**, and the entire anti-forever-emergency read [PROG PERPETUAL-MOTION,
 NO FINISH LINE] depends on **feeling not being able to reclassify itself as fact on
 demand**. Close that door here and the measurement layer holds; leave it open and every
 pin is negotiable by whoever feels most.
 **Falsification:** a finding, remedy, or mute resting on **distress as its evidence** =
 pathos-as-fact = failed [rhyme HARM VS FEELING: remedy requires act + damage]. A **priority
 set by feeling** = **legitimate** [not a violation]. A rule enforced **only against one
 side's pathos** = register asymmetry = failed.

 UNMARKED MIX [demonic / risky -- routing without steering]:
 **[A] Pathos → logos costume** -- feeling routed through institutional grammar
 [humanitarian, democracy, protection, science, "natural life" press line]. Risk:
 audience obeys **virtue grammar**; cost and contact ledger stay **off-stage**.
 **[B] Logos → pathos costume** -- instrument read used to end the conversation
 [chart, plea term, "never released" headline]. Risk: **shuts down** warranted
 dread without engaging the trade.
 **[C] Spectacle blend** -- comeuppance hunger + family moral verdict + nudge
 conspiracy in one beat. Risk: **execution porn** or **betrayal porn** posing
 as civic seriousness.

 **Demon** here = **terminal unmarked mix** that **always** absorbs falsification
 into feeling or data costume [institutional or deployer player] -- not "having
 emotions" or "using statistics."

 QUIET VS LOUD PATHOS [ethics split -- same fuel]:

 mock family plea, parole panic on **wrong legal object**, women's-prison panic
 as **SKU** -- pathos **on mic** for discharge.

 appetite; **no trust** that elite mercy machinery keeps **natural life** honest

 beat?** If neither pathos nor logos is labeled and the move demands compliance
 → **HOLD** [mixed membrane]. Mixed analysis in one head is fine; **mixed sale**

ORG GEOMETRY — SMALL/MID PRODUCTION STRUCTURE [analyst policy -- chat
 2026-07-24]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst personal org-design preference -- not
 framework finding. General economic org template for small/mid scale
 not big-business geometry. Rhymes AEC ORG/FIRM [CC without CEO
 permission; exit + contract + correction -- not strategy ballots].

 ONE-LINE:
 Bottom production holds support; reps win by availability + highest
 support; ranked seats safe until last; top command set by reps at 51%
 mid admin clerical only; no elections.

 SCALE:
 **Can scale to big/large** in theory [geometry does not forbid it] --
 analyst **does not want** that path. Operative preference: **small or
 mid only**. Not Fortune-scale pyramid by choice, not by impossibility.
 Nation-band anti-monopoly / breakup of big slop corps = market niches --
 org discipline still local.

 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 THREE TIERS [bottom / mid / top]
 --------------------------------------------------------------------------

 BOTTOM = **production**
 MID = **admin**
 TOP = **command**

 BOTTOM [production]:
 Makes the thing. Every worker holds exactly **one support** -- analyst
 term for **active delegable standing**. Support is **not a vote** and
 **not an election** [no campaigns, no ballot theater, no yap=standing].
 Worker **actively** delegates support to one rep at a time; revocable
 on term or breach. Non-transferable except delegate-to-rep.

 **Reps** [bottom governance -- not a separate tier]:
 Production representatives -- not managers, not logistics staff, not
 mid admin. Win open seats by:
 [1] **Available** [eligible, accepts duty, no conflict/disqualifier]
 [2] **Highest support** among available candidates for that seat
 Ranked by total support held each cycle. Rep weight = supports
 collected.

 SEAT STABILITY [load-bearing -- safe ≠ protected]:
 Reps ranked #1, #2, #3 … by support. **#1 safe, #2 safe, etc.** --
 each rank above the tail **does not shift out** this cycle.
 **Safe = not shifting out** -- not immunity, not above recall, not
 permanent. Lose rank → lose safe status next cycle.
 **Last rank only = at risk** -- sole seat that can shift out to an
 available higher-support challenger. Not mass turnover; one tail
 contest per cycle unless body size changes.

 Reps **set command** at **>50% rep-seat majority**. Reps **recall
 command** on same bar for documented charter breach. Binding charter
 actions [surplus split, routing boundary, charter amendment] require
 **>50% rep-seat majority** -- not daily ops.
 **Rep count mirrors command count** [|reps| = |command|] -- balanced
 geometry, not pyramid.

 TOP [command -- small plural, no mass addition]:
 **Default lean: single person** -- may be **multiple** when workload
 requires, but **no mass addition** [no swelling council, no holacracy
 blob]. Hard cap: small bounded group only [analyst band: 1--few; not
 department-scale command]. **Command** = authority inside published
 charter: contracts, external boundary, hire/fire below command, daily
 priorities, appoint mid admin. Accountable to rep body. Takes seat when
 reps ratify named person[s] at 51%. Loses seat on rep recall at 51%.

 MID [admin]:
 Appointed by command. **No authority** -- records, scheduling, compliance
 forms, payroll logs. Routes information; cannot assign work, approve
 spend, hire/fire, or rate people. Shadow-boss via paperwork gate =
 failure mode.

 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 SUPPORT [term discipline]
 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Support = one **active** delegable unit per production worker. **Not
 vote.** **Not election.** Standing token moved by deliberate delegate
 -- counted support only, no campaign lane. Rhymes yap≠vote at
 nation scale; org-scale parallel only. **Partial rhyme** the **tribe-geometry
 support ledger** [support ledger, voluntary delegate, no approval gate -- not
 identity; **no enforcement-medium / vow physics in IRL org**].

 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 EXTRACTION KILL RULE [load-bearing -- chat 2026-07-24]
 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 If it **stinks of dishonest extraction** → **no**. **No thoughts beyond
 that.** No debate, steelman, pilot, or "industry standard." Do not adopt
 do not negotiate; do not pilot. Full stop.

 **Sniff test** [any one = rot]: axis hidden [who benefits by behavioral
 record unnamed]; yap > doing [mission without published surplus split]
 soft beneficiary without veto test; meter forgery [visibility over output]
 asymmetric cost [floor pays, command doesn't]; divisive symbol fight with
 routing unchanged; complexity moat [can't trace money/people moves]
 recall blocked; election creep; safe → protected. Needs a paragraph to
 defend → stinks.

 **Charter one-line:** Dishonest extraction detected → reject on sight.

 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 WHAT IS NOT IN SCOPE
 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Elections, candidacy theater, strategy ballots on product roadmap.
 Firm-wide democracy cosplay [AEC anti-pattern]. Separate support-staff
 class / personal attendants. Mid admin with soft command. Safe misread
 as protected [#1+ immune from recall]. Command mass expansion.

 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 FAILURE MODES
 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Election creep [campaigns, slates, propaganda] → reject; support is
 active delegate only.
 Safe misread as protected → #1 still recallable for cause at 51%.
 51% on daily ops → paralysis; charter-list only.
 Command council swells → violates no-mass-addition cap.
 Same person holds rep + command seat → blocked.
 Mid admin blocks workflow → records only.

 FALSIFICATION:
 Binding charter change without rep 51% → meter broken. Safe seat
 survives documented breach without recall path → protected crept in.
 Command headcount grows without analyst charter revision → mass addition.

 Cross-ref: AEC FUNDAMENTALS ORG/FIRM band; ECONOMIC CHARTER [below]; POLICY BULLETS Unions
 ; CONTRIBUTOR LEDGER AND FRANCHISE
 [corp no franchise; yap≠vote]; HOUSEHOLD ORG [no sun-orbit]; OPEN FLAT PROFIT AT ORG MEMBRANE
 FRAMEWORK TRANSMISSION STRATEGY [downstream org selector]; **tribe-geometry
 support ledger** [partial rhyme -- source name not used in policy].
 T3 Medium: rep body default size, cycle length, top plural cap number
 open.
