EXEC COMP AND BUYBACK CONFLICT ROWS [economic charter A/E extension -- chat 2026-11-05]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst membrane vocabulary -- not framework finding.
 **Home under:** ECONOMIC CHARTER rows A [surplus split] + E [exec liability].
 Extends the charter row set; does not create a new unit class.

 ONE-LINE:
 **Equity comp allowed; own-unit conflicts banned; payout measured net of
 externalized cost; buyback is a surplus-split decision, not exec discretion.**

 RULES:
 **[1] HOLDING OK -- CONFLICTS BANNED.** Deployers may hold unit equity. Banned,
 full stop, no incentive function: **hedging** own-unit exposure; **pledging** unit
 equity as loan collateral; **acceleration** of vesting on buyback / merger /
 restructuring / spin; **sale into the unit's own repurchase window**.
 **[2] PAYOUT NET OF EXTERNALIZED COST.** Vesting + clawback keyed to **rework cost**,
 **safety findings**, **labor-share decline**, **exit-cost construction** -- not
 accounting restatement alone [rhyme EFFORT-QUALITY-COST COUPLING; applied_s6
 SHAREHOLDER PRIMACY RESTORATION; CORRECTION THEATRE].
 **[3] BUYBACK AT ROW D BAR.** Repurchase authorization = **amendment-class decision**
 [same bar as command install/recall], not exec discretion. Insider participation
 disclosed **pre-trade**. No repurchase while a row C correction breach is unresolved.
 **[4] HORIZON.** Vest **>= tenure**; mandatory hold **N years post-exit**; no
 time-vest-only grants at **large** class.
 **[5] PURE CASH NOT A FIX [reject].** Blanket cash-only comp removes the residual
 claim = removes the cost signal [soft-budget / soviet-firm read] and re-encodes as
 phantom equity, LTIP, deferred comp, 401(k) match stock, or family-trust holding.
 **Instrument record:** IRC 162(m) 1993 [cash capped $1M, performance-based exempt]
 -> options conversion within 1--2 yr, pay rose after the cap aimed at it; Japan
 pre-1997 options ban inside a **keiretsu package** [not the ban alone] + 1997
 reform -> >1/3 TSE adoption in 7 yr; EU CRD IV bonus cap -> fixed-pay + role-based
 allowance shift with regulator chasing reclassification.

 corp-form units sit outside rows A--E; they need a separate **statutory** instrument

PERSONALIST SUCCESSION VS STATE DISSOLUTION [analyst policy -- chat 2026-11-05; bias-labeled]

 finding, not a forecast of any named state's collapse.

 **Split [load-bearing]:** **regime rupture** and **state dissolution** are separate events
 with separate causes; collapse talk merges them and gets both wrong. **Regime rupture:**
 near-certain inside a 25-year window where the apex holds office by personal network with
 **no succession procedure**. Armed factions [security services, national guard, ministry
 patronage] are **competing clients**, not one service that can hand off power -- each has
 learned arbitration is fatal. Apex clearing = **uncontrolled event**. **State dissolution:**
 **periphery event**, not center event. Test = whether a weakened center still holds
 **federal units with distinct nation or religion** -- not who wins the capital.

 **Historical base rate:** 1953 Beria, 1964 Khrushchev, 1991 coup, 1993 parliament --
 state **survived three of four**. The one that did not dissolved because **republics
 walked**, not because a Kremlin elite scramble broke it. Apply the same split to any
 personalist federation.

 **Predictive form [not mood]:** state **persists** = high; regime **changes** = high;
 territory **lost** = real tail risk, routed through **fiscal transfer capacity + ethnic
 federal structure**. "Might not exist" without the periphery mechanism named is
 **unfalsifiable doom register** [rhyme VCF test; Personal ZOOM IN/OUT out-scale doom].

 **Falsifier:** personalist apex system with **no** succession procedure survives **two**
 apex transitions **≥10 years apart** with **no** armed-faction arbitration and **no**
 periphery exit attempt → split read weakens.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Comparative read on why some historical enmities close and
 others stay live -- not framework finding, not a moral ranking of nations.

 **Claim:** "every nation cultivates memory" is true and empty -- the variable is not
 virtue but whether a **settlement architecture** exists. Europe did not transcend its
 history by being better; it **purchased closure with sovereignty**. Four instruments,
 all load-bearing, none substitutable by apology:

 | Instrument | What it prices |
 |---|---|
 | **Territorial finality** [treaty-fixed borders, no live revisionist claim] | removes the object of the dispute |
 | **Economic integration** [trade/monetary entanglement] | makes war materially irrational for the integrating class |
 | **Supranational legal supremacy** [court both sides actually accept] | gives a forum that can rule against a member |
 | **Guilt-constitution** [state identity built on the crime, not a negotiating position] | makes revision politically impossible at home |

 **Blocked pairs lack all four.** No territorial finality [live island/mainland/border
 claims], no accepted court, no shared guilt-constitution -- so the apology is always
 **revisable by the next cabinet**, and memory politics stay a **domestic faction weapon**
 rather than a settled state position. Nationalism also needs an out-group, and the
 nearest one is a neighbor with a disputed feature.

 **Do not read as:** Asia uniquely vindictive, or Europe morally superior. Read as:
 **closure is expensive and someone must surrender sovereignty to get it**; where no one
 will, the friction stays **low-grade** rather than industrial -- boycotts, visa fights,
 textbook rows, slurs, awkward family dinners. Low-grade chauvinism is human default
 everywhere; what varies is whether a **state has an interest in heating it**.

 **Falsifier:** blocked pair with **no** territorial dispute, **no** accepted court, **no**
 guilt-constitution, yet **no** state-level memory politics over **≥20 years** → architecture
 read weakens. Or: settled pair where closure arrived **without** any of the four
 instruments → instrument list is wrong.

 Cross-ref: CIVILIZATIONAL FRICTION VS ESSENTIALIST INCOMPATIBILITY [contact scale --
 sibling]; PERSONALIST SUCCESSION VS STATE DISSOLUTION; ANTI-WAR EXCEPT MANDATED TOTAL WAR.
 T3 Medium: comparative structural read; Low as regional forecast.

**INFORMAL VS CONTINENTAL EMPIRE [child of settlement architecture -- chat 2026-11-05]:**
 Two empire types, different legitimacy problems; do not merge.
 **Informal [US-shaped]:** basing, currency, alliance hierarchy, extraterritorial reach;
 **metropole and periphery separated by a boundary** -- franchise real **inside** the line,
 suspended **outside** [Puerto Rico / territory tier = the seam]. Democracy is therefore
 **not pure costume**: it is real within a border drawn somewhere else, and legitimacy
 remains **constitutive of the people**, so the story-vs-routing gap is a **live domestic
 scandal** with somewhere to go.
 **Continental [PRC-shaped]:** inherits prior-dynasty territory, so the periphery sits
 **inside the same border** as the metropole; **no exterior where franchise ends** because
 the empire **is** the country. Legitimacy is **doctrinal + performance** [party leadership,
 growth, order, sovereignty restoration] with a civilizational register -- "democracy" is a
 **redefined term of art**, not a claim to competitive elections. Costume is **thinner**,
 not thicker; the gap is **not a betrayal** because no popular-sovereignty promise was made.
 **Shared:** "the people" as legitimating word with **no use-audit** -- that is the actual
 commonality, and it rhymes order_truth MID ENACTMENT [expression ≠ enactment].
 Cross-ref: MID ENACTMENT; MANAGED DEMOCRACY VS REAL DEMOCRACY DEFINITION; applied
 POLITICS legitimacy source [consent real or manufactured] -- **not** a democracy census.
 T3 Low: rhyme-grade structural read; primary-text verify before any nation claim.

AUSTERITY COALITION HOLLOWING [analyst policy -- chat 2026-11-05; bias-labeled]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Structural mechanism read on fiscal squeeze -- not framework
 finding, not a prediction of any named election.

 **Mechanism [load-bearing]:** "less democracy" does **not** follow from austerity as
 **fewer elections**. It follows from **losing the surplus that buys off losers**. While
 surplus flows, coalition-building is **positive-sum** -- every faction can be paid
 something, and elections can **settle** by distributing. When surplus closes, coalition
 becomes **zero-sum**: every faction must **win outright** because losing means real loss,
 not a smaller share. Result = **hollowing**: elections still held, still contested, and
 **unable to settle anything**. Frozen majorities + permanent campaign + recall-as-routine.

 **Second leg -- overstretch asymmetry:** military commitments are fixed by **threat
 perception** while revenue falls, so the cut lands on **everything that does not lobby**.
 Defense and entitlement are protected; discretionary, maintenance, and correction
 capacity absorb the adjustment. That **degrades the correction machinery first** -- the
 exact instrument needed to respond to the squeeze.

 **Do not read as:** austerity causes authoritarianism. Read as: austerity removes the
 **distributional slack** that makes coalition politics non-terminal, and removes it
 **fastest from the repair organs**.

 **Falsifier:** fiscal squeeze **with** credible surplus replacement [growth, redistribution,
 external transfer] yet coalition fragmentation and recall-routinization still rise →
 surplus-buyoff leg weakens. Or: austerity period where discretionary/correction capacity
 rises while military + entitlement hold → overstretch leg wrong.

 Cross-ref: MANAGED DEMOCRACY VS REAL DEMOCRACY DEFINITION; CENTRAL UNION AND RECALL
 ARCHITECTURE; MONEY AND FRAMEWORK ORIENTATION [roll-debt leg]; applied EXTRACTION CYCLE.
 T3 Medium: mechanism read; Low as electoral forecast.

**IMPERIAL CATEGORY AND DIASPORA ROUTING [child -- chat 2026-11-05]:** "Asian" is a
 **Western administrative artifact** [census box, airport-lounge tier] covering ~4.5B
 people; inside Asia the operating axes are **nation, language, religion, caste, region** --
 the pan-category is **not an in-group**, so "why do they dislike each other" has no
 referent. What is live instead, as **specific pairs at specific temperatures**:
 [a] **unresolved war memory kept instrumented by domestic politics**; [b] **colonial
 administration hierarchies outliving the colonizers** [color/race stratification,
 martial-race recruitment, favored-minority commercial roles → anti-diaspora riot
 **economics**, not culture clash]; [c] **young nation-state borders** [1940s–60s;
 nationalism needs a nearby out-group]; [d] in the **diaspora**, **the lumping itself
 manufactures rivalry** [one institutional slot → zero-sum competition between subgroups
 with no prior relationship]. Blunt high-context in-group register also **reads as
 hostility to outsiders** -- register artifact, not enmity. **Falsifier:** category with
 **no** shared state, language, religion, or legal system yet **uniform** mutual hostility
 across pairs → category-invalidity read weakens; or hostility tracks **colonial instrument
 presence** rather than any cultural variable → instrument leg is the live one.
 Cross-ref: CIVILIZATIONAL FRICTION VS ESSENTIALIST INCOMPATIBILITY; INSULT REFERENT
 REGISTER; POSTWAR SETTLEMENT ARCHITECTURE.
 T3 Low: category critique is structural; pair-level temperatures are **source-dependent**.

HONESTY OVER COSTUME [analyst policy -- chat 2026-07-29]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst epistemic axiom -- not framework finding,
 not endorsement of harm. Operative preference for orientation under
 uncertainty; runs before regime jerseys and after Boundary B
 civilians.

 **AXIOM [operative]:**
 Prefer **legible harm** over **illegible harm dressed as good**.
 Scaled dishonesty ranks worse than named bad -- not because lies are
 magically worse than force, but because **costume at scale forecloses
 measurement, correction, and consent**. Analyst will always want
 something honest even if "evil," because something dishonest and huge
 is a greater evil.

 **LIE = UNBOUNDED LIABILITY:**
 Lying is not one false sentence -- it starts a **maintenance stack**:
 cover the gap, cover the cover, punish near-seers, build institutions
 that enforce the story. General pattern: hiding an **infinite effort**
 to hide something worse. Hidden root = unbounded liability; cannot
 price, plan, or correct what cannot be audited.

 **HONEST BAD = QUOTED PRICE:**
 Honest bad may still be bad -- but **stated terms ≈ actual terms**.
 Know what you get; surprise is mostly failure not product. Bounded
 trade inside a legible lane beats beautiful lease with hidden
 accelerators. Not cynicism -- **contract clarity** and epistemic
 survival.

LIGHT SHADOW FALSE LIGHT PSYCHE DOCTRINE [analyst policy -- analyst chat 2026-10-16

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst psyche doctrine -- not framework finding.
 Not clinical advice. Operative read for **self-steering under crisis** when
 full shadow-integration project is off the table.

 THESIS [one line]:
 **Everyone runs imbalance under the hood; conscious self = light as bearing;
 shadow = permanent crew -- coexist, deploy on task, never sell false light.**

 OPERATIVE DEFS:
 **Light** = orientation + limits [values, repair, whom you protect, chosen
 direction] -- **not** purity, permanent niceness, or hireable-only persona.
 **Shadow** = disowned or heavy capacity [rage, cut, cynicism, selfishness,
 numbness, "prove them wrong" fuel, threat-read, grind when hope is dead] --
 always present; not a bug to delete this quarter.
 **Honest dual** = light at wheel most days; shadow in engine room or **time-
 boxed mission** [boundary, exit, survival grind, cold read] then stand down.
 **False light** = **owned** backend [they run and defend the dark] + **hidden**
 public face [brand, virtue, safety story] -- concealment is **the product**,
 not failed integration. Corp-scale = type instance [rhyme institutional
 shadow-steering -- not identity claim on every employee].
 **No light** = shadow as **identity** [open domination, harm without repair,
 conscience ornamental] -- **demonic** = metaphor for total possession; may be
 **unwilled** [compulsion, wiring, addiction -- not chosen villainy] -- harm
 floor + exit still apply.
 **Crisis-normal** = no planetary peace truce assumed; coexist/deploy = **ops
 tempo**, not celebration of permanent inner war -- **local stand-down** still
 [sleep, one non-spite win, one honest bond] when billing allows.

 OPERATING CONDITIONS [analyst rebuttal bundle -- chat 2026-10-16 amend]:
 **Primary goal** = survive [rent, food, gatekeeping interfaces] while **avoiding
 harm to others** -- shadow fuel licensed under that pair, not sport or identity.
 **Harm floor** includes **slow self-harm** [burnout, bitterness, neglect] --
 not only strangers.
 **Universal imbalance ≠ equal level** -- everyone carries shadow; **harm, power,
 and blame are graded** ["we all have darkness" does not excuse abuse].
 **False-light system** = **apex makes the membrane** [owned-hide interface +
 defended backend]; downstream = plumbing -- not pure structure-without-actor.
 **Structural rig** = psyche doctrine steers **inside** rigged game [jobs, health,
 geography, discrimination] -- does not replace political/economic read.
 **Shadow deploy** = **task-dependent** [cut, grind, threat-read vs docs, allies,
 boring repetition] -- beast is specialist, not default solver.
 **Public throttle** = same ethics as survival [2]: harm reduction + gates, not
 personal sainthood performance -- long mask is **tax**, not hypocrisy by default.
 **Integration crusade** = luxury good when rent due -- coexistence minimum bar
 stands; falsify if coexistence becomes **permanent avoidance** of needed care.

 MODE LADDER [which row am I in?]:
 | Mode | Face | Engine | Analyst stance |
 |------|------|--------|----------------|
 | Honest dual | Real limits; some transparency to self | Shadow on task | **Target** under crisis |
 | False light | Manufactured virtue / stability | Owned hidden control | **Oppose** at scale; **refuse self** |
 | No light | None or mockery | Shadow as god | **Hard floor** -- harm limits + exit |

 CRISIS GEARBOX [when life is already on fire]:

 hyper-visible hustle gate] -- **valid fuel** if steered.
 **Command** picks one crisis line + one daily shipped output; **beast** grinds
 ugly tasks; **throttle** on interfaces [interview, bridge-burning tone].
 **Cannot unleash fully** = strategic [employability, wrong-target risk] not
 cowardice.
 **Anti-patterns [stupid version -- reject]:** "be worst sometimes" as lifestyle;
 shadow as moral excuse; integration theater while beast hijacks; performing
 saint outside while denying engine inside [personal false light].

 COEXISTENCE VS FIX:
 **Coexistence** = know the pattern, truce, bounded channels, repair spills --

 **Fix-the-shadow crusade** optional; **shrink blast radius** and **less

 [f] Universal-shadow rhetoric used to flatten harm -> reassert graded power +

OUTSOURCE IMPORT TARIFF GATE [analyst policy -- analyst chat 2026-08-16
 ; rate tier pass 6]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst analyst policy -- not framework
 finding. Operative labor-routing tariff from Halo outsource economics
 thread. **Not a ban** -- dual import tariff; **30% boomerang** rate
 selects for **high-value-only** contract-out-and-return.

 CORE METER [one sentence]:
 **Normal import = 20%.** **Boomerang outsourcing = 30%** on returned
 outsource value at org membrane [contract out + return]. Commodity /
 volume vendor routing priced out; scarce specialist lane may still clear
 at 30%. **Partner / co-dev label irrelevant** -- substance over
 contract grammar.

 DEFINITIONS [minimal]:
 **Normal import [20%]:** goods / services / materials crossing the
 trade membrane on standard import lane -- not publisher labor-routing
 boomerang [consumption stack, parts, ordinary cross-border supply].
 **Boomerang outsourcing [30%]:** spec or work leaves org control →
 external hands execute → **deliverable returns** for integration
 [art pack, mission blockout, engineering milestone, cinematic pass].
 Functional import -- not port-of-entry geography; not finished-SKU
 fiction. **This is the Halo / AAA publisher-hub pattern.**
 **Outsourced:** publisher-directed spec + external execution + returned
 build artifact. Captive studio, "strategic partner," or co-dev doing
 core build = boomerang routing fact.
 **High-value-only [survivor lane at 30%]:** boomerang rational only when
 external capacity is **non-substitutable specialist** [scarce skill,
 one-off instrument, capacity gap you cannot staff in-window] -- not
 commodity labor arbitrage or multi-vendor polish flood.
 **Tariff base:** **returned outsource value** at contract milestone
 [invoice tied to deliverable, not generic retainer hide].

 RATE [load-bearing -- two tiers]:
 **20%** -- normal import tariff [standard cross-border lane].
 **30%** -- **boomerang outsourcing** [contract-out-and-return].
 **Not prohibition** -- not sector ban, not zero lane. Higher tier
 targets rent-the-hands routing: **15-studio commodity stacks** [AAA
 publisher-hub model] lose harder to employed core build + coordination
 cost; genuine specialist buy may still clear at 30%.
 **Defense:** same **30%** boomerang gate [no volume-outsource carve-out].
 National-security programs may still use **high-value-only** external
 return under boomerang tier; commodity routing not exempt.

 ORG BOUNDARY [rhyme OPEN FLAT PROFIT AT ORG MEMBRANE]:
 **30%** runs when **boomerang deliverable crosses back** into publisher /
 prime integrator membrane. Internal FTE handoffs not boomerang events.
 Sham partner labels do not reset meter to 20%.

 MECHANISM INTENT:
 Collapse **variable-labor fiction** [HR savings without production
 savings]. Make **employ-the-hands** competitive with **rent-the-hands**
 for core build. **20% / 30% split** -- ordinary trade continues
 labor boomerang pays premium. Negative specimen: Halo Campaign Evolved
 ~15 co-dev credits + post-ship internal cuts.

 ENFORCEMENT [desk]:
 Direction audit: who owned spec, who executed, what returned. Reclassify
 boomerang as normal import → dodge → problems lane [rhyme OPEN FLAT
 PROFIT AT ORG MEMBRANE dodge tier].

 FALSIFICATION [policy coherence]:
 30% boomerang + employment boundary still yields Evolved-scale commodity
 vendor stack at lower total cost than in-house → weakens gate.
 Specialist-only boomerang disappears entirely → 30% may be too high
 or audit too blunt.

 Cross-ref: OPEN FLAT PROFIT AT ORG MEMBRANE [org membrane rhyme]; POLICY BULLETS
 Labor; ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [applied]

applied shareholder-primacy [S6 grep].

 **MOTIVE IS PATHOS, HARM IS REAL [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05; load-bearing counter
 discipline]:** **motive is only pathos.** A motive claim is **not a finding and not a
 rebuttal.** **Harm is real** -- it is **on the record**, in output, cost, and who was
 hurt. **So every counter is judged on two questions and only these two:**
 | Question | Lane | Valid as rebuttal? |
 |----------|------|--------------------|
 | **What did it do?** [output, extraction, stand-down, cheaper fix refused, who billed] | **HARM / IO** | **YES** -- this is the only real rebuttal |
 | **Why did they do it / what kind of person are they / what do objectors usually want** | **MOTIVE / PATHOS** | **NO** -- unless the objection itself **rests** on motive |
 **What this bans outright [as rebuttal]:** any reply whose content is **a claim about the
 objector rather than about the thing objected to** -- their consistency, their motives,
 their class, their tone, their sincerity. **Each is a counter-objection, not an answer**, and
 **counter-objection is the loop**: objecting to the objection **leaves the original harm
 claim untouched**. **One line:** *if the reply can be made without ever looking at the
 output, it is not a reply.*
 **The one legitimate use of motive:** when the **objection itself depends on claimed
 motive** [*"he's a good man so it isn't extraction,"* *"we meant well,"* *"the critics are
 just jealous"*], **then** motive is the actual claim and may be answered at that level.
 **Answering motive with motive is symmetric; answering harm with motive is evasion.**
 **Symmetry binds both directions:** you may not dismiss a critic as **"just bitter"** and
 may not dismiss a defender as **"just greedy."** **Both are pathos.** The test is the same
 for an ally and an enemy [rhyme COUNTER-ROW DISCIPLINE IO-not-intentions; THEORY-OF-THINGS
 #8 prove-DEME].
 **Why this is the strongest counter rule in the file:** **motive is unfalsifiable and harm
 is not.** A motive rebuttal can never be checked, so it can never be wrong, so it can never
 teach anything. **Harm claims can be checked** -- output, ledger, docket, floor panel.
 **A stack that argues in motives cannot audit itself** [rhyme HARM VS FEELING: feeling is
 real and chosen; harm is documented].
 **Falsification:** a rebuttal that **dismisses an objection by characterizing the objector**
 without showing the objection **rests** on that characterization = **motive-as-rebuttal** =
 failed. A row using motive language **in place of** IO evidence = failed. A rule applied to
 **critics' motives but not to defenders'** = asymmetric pathos = failed.

MUNICIPAL PROCUREMENT THRESHOLD AND VENDOR PATTERN ENFORCEMENT [analyst policy -- chat 2026-10-24]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst operative position -- not framework finding.
 Trigger: municipal/vendor contracts priced epsilon below charter, council, or
 competitive-bidding caps [ALPR / Flock-class surveillance; national epsilon-
 under-threshold playbook on public record].

 ONE-LINE:
 **Discretionary spend cap = $5,000; above that = public process on record;
 repeated epsilon-under-cap pattern = substance over form -- looks like crime,
 treat as crime.**

 CORE STACK [operative]:
 [1] **Floor:** no sole-source or executive/manager signature **> $5,000**
 without posted notice + public record.
 [2] **Process:** above floor -- intent posted **before** award; vendor, scope,
 term, justification; comment window; council vote or competitive bid per local
 membrane.
 [3] **Aggregation:** rolling **12-month** total per vendor + program + related
 entity; options, renewals, change orders = **one project**.
 [4] **Anti-split:** related scopes split to stay under cap = **constructive
 evasion** -- voidable award + referential for enforcement.
 [5] **Sensitive class:** surveillance / ALPR / public-safety platform / network
 camera programs -- **notice + justification at any dollar** [oversight bypass
 is part of the product].

 ENFORCEMENT [operative]:
 **Form is not shield.** Same vendor + same product class + epsilon below known
 local cap across deals or jurisdictions = **modus operandi**, not compliance
 trivia. **Parallel tracks:** civil [void, clawback, debarment, charter breach]
 and criminal referral where officials or vendors **knew** the threshold and
 **designed** the number to avoid council or bidding [false certification,
 conspiracy, wire/mail in furtherance -- desk refers to counsel; not legal
 advice]. Rhyme SCI POLICY POLITICS ROUTING: politics-as-spectacle without
 procurement on record is extraction; this block is **government rules**.

 FALSIFICATION [analyst would revise if]: jurisdictions with **$5k floor +
 aggregation + anti-split** show **no** measurable shift to split-invoice or
 change-order evasion over 24+ months **and** vendor epsilon-pricing disappears
 without enforcement action.

 HARM VS FEELING [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05; bias-labeled operative position]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PERSONAL BIAS WARNING. Analyst policy -- not framework finding, not a claim about
 clinical conditions, not a license against anyone. Bounds **what the state remedies**,
 not **what people are allowed to feel**.

 ONE-LINE:
 **Feeling bad about something is a choice; literal harm is harm.** The state prices
 **act + damage**; it does not price **the reaction**. Both halves are load-bearing --
 the second prevents dismissal, the first prevents an infinite claim spigot.
 **But a feeling is not nothing:** the state may **note** it -- a **small mark**, then
 the person makes **other choices**. **Mark ≠ remedy.**

 **MARK VS REMEDY [distinction that resolves the reputation collision]:** the state has
 **two instruments**, not one:
 | Instrument | Output | Priced on | Limits |
 |------------|--------|-----------|--------|
 | **Mark** [note] | public **record** the event happened [flag, finding, listing] | **the event on record** | **no money**, **no job ban**, **no compelled apology**, **no transfer to the aggrieved** |
 | **Remedy** [damages] | **compensation / injunction / prosecution** | **documented harm** | requires the **harm test** below |

 **Mark is how a feeling gets acknowledged without becoming a remedy.** A feeling is
 **noted** [the violence-bait shame register, a conduct finding, a published adjudication]
 and then **the person makes other choices** -- avoid, speak, exit, refuse, stigmatize.
 **No one is paid because they felt bad;** the mark tells third parties **what happened**,
 and third parties route themselves.
 **UNBOUNDED THIRD-PARTY CONSEQUENCE IS THE POINT [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** the
 state does **not** cap, audit, or license what private parties do with a published
 finding. **No** list of permitted consequences, **no** employer permission, **no** "may
 not refuse service" rule, **no** proportionality board. **A mark is information**; how
 people spend information is **theirs** [rhyme HARM VS FEELING: the reaction is the
 person's, not the state's]. **Do not build a mark-compliance layer** -- a state that
 polices reactions to its own findings has become the reputation ministry it was
 supposed to replace.
 **CONTEST PROCESS [fix -- chat 2026-11-05; a *process*, not a cap]:** because the
 consequence is deliberately unbounded, the **finding must be contestable** -- the only
 thing that can be wrong is the **record**, so the record is what gets corrected:
 **[m1] Notice:** the marked person is **told the finding, the row, and the evidence**
 before publication [or immediately upon emergency publication -- violence-bait timing].
 **[m2] Contest on request:** a **rebuttal right**, **free** [no filing fee -- rhyme
 NO FEE ON OVERSIGHT], with **access to the evidence relied on**.
 **[m3] Two grounds only:** **factually wrong** [the event did not happen as found] **or
 **procedurally defective** [no notice, no hearing, wrong row]. **Not a ground:** "I
 dislike the consequence," "it cost me money," or "people overreacted" -- those are
 **third-party routing**, which is the point.
 **[m4] Correction or affirmation is published:** the finding is **withdrawn with equal
 prominence** or **affirmed with the rebuttal attached**. **Silent removal is not
 permitted** [a vanished mark leaves third parties unable to tell "wrong" from
 "expunged"].
 **[m5] Erroneous mark = state error, not state intent** [STATE-ERROR LANE]: correction +
 restoration + **no mark on the person** for having contested; the **officer who
 published a known-false finding** takes the harm lane.
 **[m6] Sunset** [rhyme VIOLENCE BAIT sunset listing]: every mark carries a **published
 expiry**; renewal requires a **new finding**, not inertia.
 **Do not merge with the cap:** contesting tests **whether the record is true**. It never
 tests **whether the consequence was proportionate** -- proportion is **not** the state's
 question once the finding stands.
 **Falsification:** a mark published with **no contest path** = failed. A contest that
 **costs money** = fee gate on the record = failed. A mark **quietly removed** with no
 public correction = failed [third parties cannot audit]. A "consequence review board"
 or proportionality appeal appearing = **reputation ministry** = failed.
 **Rhyme existing homes:** VIOLENCE BAIT = **mark, not damages** [civil tier: nominal or
 community service only to **stamp the finding**, not to compensate]; `reputation =
 liquidity` is **why a mark has teeth** for serious actors -- **not** a claim that
 reputational loss is **compensable harm**.

 **HALF 1 -- feeling bad is a choice [not a remedy lane]:**
 **Emotional response is agency, not injury.** Being **offended, uncomfortable,
 insulted, or upset** by **speech, argument, mockery, or a described reality** is a
 **choice the person makes** and a **cost they carry** [rhyme TOOL-USE: cost is never
 zero, but it is **theirs**]. ⇒ **not a harm class**, **not a state remedy**, **not a
 prosecution lane**, **not an HR escalation lane**.
 **Corollary -- offense is not evidence.** A claim that something **is** harm because
 someone **feels** harmed is **circular**: feelings follow **whatever a person believes
 they should feel**, and belief is exactly what is in dispute [rhyme RESOURCE PANEL GAMING:
 the meter is the feeling, so the answer is always yes].
 **Asshole register stays legal** [VIOLENCE BAIT [1]: "asshole OK" -- no state mark for
 mere jerk]. **Stigma stays culture**, both directions [CONTEXT MOBILIZATION FAIRNESS].
 **Not "be nice":** the rule is **no remedy**, not **no consequence**.

 **HALF 2 -- literal harm is harm [do not dilute it]:**
 **Measurable damage to body, property, livelihood, access, or conduct record is a harm
 class** regardless of how it is **spoken about**, **who names it**, or **whether the
 framing is fashionable**. **Physical injury, sexual violation, theft, fraud, coercion,
 confinement, deprivation of floor, and retaliation** are **harm** -- **fully
 remediable**, **not softened** by the fact that feelings are separately not a claim.
 **Do not run the trick in reverse:** "that's just your feelings" is **not** a defense
 against **actual damage**. A person can be **offended and also robbed**; the offense
 part is theirs, the robbery part is the state's. **Separate the lanes, do not collapse
 either direction.**
 **Rhyme "hurt is hurt"** [economic charter STEEL [14][22]]: **harm is judged on the act
 and its damage**, not on the sentiment attached to either side.

 **THE TEST [cheap, do not complicate]:**
 **Can it be pointed at, documented, and shown on a body/property/ledger?** **Yes** →
 **harm lane**, full remedy. **No** → **feeling lane**: **no remedy**, but the event may
 still get a **mark** [see MARK VS REMEDY] and the person uses speech, exit, refusal, and
 stigma [NATION EXIT LANE]. **Not** a test of **severity of feeling**; **not** a test of
 **who is complaining**.
 **Remedy is earned by proof, not by category:** **if a person proves harm they may get
 something; if they cannot, they get nothing.** The **burden sits on the claimant**, and
 the output scales to **what is proven** -- **no proof, no payout** [rhyme [CASE] standing
 + evidence; FAIR LAW]. **Mark is not contingent on proof of damage** -- a **mark** records
 that a **finding was made**; a **remedy** requires that **damage was shown**.

 **WHAT THIS DOES NOT SAY:**
 - **Not** that feelings are fake or optional in ordinary life -- they are **real and
 chosen**, which is precisely why they are **the person's cost** and not a **state
 claim**. **Real and chosen is not an insult:** noting a feeling as chosen says **the
 person is the one who decides what to do next**, not that the feeling was trivial.
 - **TRUE MENTAL ILLNESS IS NOT A CHOICE [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05]:** **genuine mental
 illness is a mental curse, not a decision.** It **cannot** be routed through the
 feeling-as-choice lane -- it is **substrate**, not reaction [rhyme TAX AXIOMS [4b]:
 physical substrate failure is not steer; same logic at the mental substrate]. **So:**
 **feeling-as-choice applies to the ordinary reaction**, **never** to disorder,
 psychosis, addiction substrate, or incapacity. **Distinct and rejected:** "I am upset
 therefore I am ill" = **category error**, and "you are ill therefore every reaction is
 involuntary" = **conscripting illness as cover** [rhyme fake-disability fraud stack].
 **Two separate lanes, do not merge in either direction.**
 - **Not** that **speech has no cost**: speech **can** cost the speaker **reputation,
 access, and audience** [stigma is culture, both directions]. **Priced routing**, not
 **verboten** -- and that cost is a **mark** effect, not a **damages** claim.

 CROSS-CHECK -- INFINITE-OFFENSE MACHINE:
 This block is the **terminus** the prog perpetual-motion read needs: **NO FINISH LINE**
 [harm class infinite at abstraction -- gaze, micro, platform edge] is only possible if
 **feeling can be reclassified as harm on demand**. **Closing the reclassification** is
 what makes **stop conditions** possible. Rhyme PROG MOBILIZATION LOOP [tone stays
 emergency because stop is forbidden]; CORPORATE REPUTATION loop [brand-safe = activation
 economy].

 FALSIFICATION:
 An **act with documented body/property/livelihood damage treated as "just feelings"**
 = **half 2 failed** [harm diluted]. An **offense, insult, or uncomfortable description
 treated as a state remedy class** = **half 1 failed** [feeling lane opened]. A remedy
 ruling that **cites the complainant's distress as the finding** rather than the **act and
 damage** = **feeling-as-evidence** = failed. Requiring **apology or affirmation** as the
 remedy output = **feeling-as-remedy** = failed. **Money or access transferred to a
 complainant with no proof of damage** = **mark/remedy collapse** = failed. **A mark
 denied because "it's only feelings"** when a finding was made = **mark suppression** =
 failed. **Genuine mental illness routed as chosen reaction** = **curse-as-choice** =
 failed.

 Cross-ref: VIOLENCE BAIT CIVIL SHAME REGISTER [asshole tier -- violence bait is act, not
 feeling]; CONTEXT MOBILIZATION VS STATUTE LAW [stigma culture / law act]; ACT-FIRST
 PUBLIC ORDER; TOOL-USE ORIENTATION [cost is real, cost is owned]; NATION EXIT LANE
 [speech + exit + stigma as non-state instruments]; PROG PERPETUAL-MOTION MORAL GAME
 [NO FINISH LINE]; economic charter STEEL [14][22] [hurt is hurt].
 T3 Medium: analyst chat synthesis 2026-11-05; remedy scope only.

OPEN WORK REGISTER [analyst -- chat 2026-11-05; named gaps, not resolved]

 PURPOSE: the policy file now carries **named holes** in its own prose. This register lists
 them in one place so nothing reads as solved by being adjacent to a solution. **Every item
 below is a real gap in the stack, stated honestly, with no fake pin standing in for it.**
 Add here; **never** bury an open item inside a topic as if closed.

 [1] FLOOR CONSTITUENCY -- ARGUMENT SET, MECHANISM NOT
 Done: WHO DEFENDS THE FLOOR gives the substrate-interest argument [the payer's own
 production depends on the floor], so the defender is a net-positive voter.
 Open: **no mechanism forces that interest to bind.** If the median voter does not feel the
 substrate bill, the floor is protected by argument only. **No pin puts the cost at the
 ballot.**

 [2] TRANSITION -- CONCEDED, NOT SEQUENCED
 TRANSITION [honest] says no easy shift. **True, and still a hole.** No order of operations
 for land, money, corp-form, franchise, and wage all repricing at once. **Anyone who loses
 knows in advance and organises first**; the file has no answer to that.

 [3] FRANCHISE-DESIGN FINDING -- NAMED, NO LANE
 Roll B shrinking while floor rows hold = "franchise-design finding... needs review"
 [RECALL CASCADE [v3]]. **No actor, no trigger, no remedy.** "Needs review" is not a lane.

 [4] PROSECUTORIAL SKEW -- NAMED, NO LANE
 Same act charged differently by district is **measured** [LAW SAME FOR POOR AND RICH [c],
 refusal dockets published] but **nothing happens when the skew appears.** Same shape as [3].

 [5] PIPELINE YIELD -- NEVER COMPUTED
 Every gate is individually justified; the **joint** yield is unknown. Nobody computes how
 much of the eligible population clears Roll A + earned positive + civic pass + net-score +
 tie revote, nor which class fails at each stage. **Correlated filters concentrate.** The
 stack cannot currently answer "what electorate does this produce."

 [6] DEATH TIER -- DISCRETION IN ONE NODE
 Automatic on conviction, no judicial downgrade, MSC bias lives at the **prosecution** layer.
 **Error cost appears nowhere in the block.** One unmonitored node carries an unbounded
 sanction; compare the file's own standard on ballots ["one ineligible vote is
 unacceptable"].

 [7] DEFAMATION -- RESOLVED
 TRUTH IS NEVER DEFAMATION settles it: a true statement cannot be defamatory, truth is a
 complete defense, and the claimant must prove falsity. **A person harmed by a true statement
 is harmed by the truth, not the speaker** -- no lane, by design. Actionable lane is **false
 statement + proven livelihood damage** only.

 [8] FLOOR PROVISION vs PROVISION-IS-CAPTURE -- ARGUED, NOT SETTLED
 The `will not parent` pin is ruled **org-band only** [WELFARE gate]. That is a **transfer
 rule**, not a proof that nation provision cannot become the same disorder instrument at
 scale. **Read as argued, not closed.**

 [9] AMENDMENT UPPER RATES -- STANDARD SET, NUMBER STILL PENDING
 District bar **set at 51% of Roll B**. **BASIC LOGIC BEFORE THE RATE** now fixes the standard
 the upper rates must meet [passable by broad support, unpassable by a faction] and requires
 the **predicted pass/fail band** to be published with the number. **Remaining:** the numbers
 themselves are still set at first ratification. Standard is no longer missing; the rate is.

 [10] CIVIC TIME BUDGET -- NOT TOTALED
 Vote + civic pass + sortition duty + jury duty + quarterly ledger + recall petitions. **No
 stated total**, and no answer to whether the load selects for people with surplus time
 [the class POOR IMMOBILITY says the stack should not recruit]. **Stipend + backfill answer
 money, not hours.**

 [11] COMPEL-PETITION POWER -- GUARDED, NOT PROPORTIONED
 [c0] bars fees; sortition rules bar packing. **Still unstated:** whether compelled
 enforcement is reviewable for **proportionality** before it fires. Capture is guarded;
 scope is not.

 [12] CONFLICT HIERARCHY -- NOT WRITTEN
 Collisions this session [dividend vs donation; coin-only vs civic pass] were resolved **in
 chat, not by rule.** The file still has **no stated priority order** among its own
 commitments. A reader cannot resolve a new collision without asking.

 HOW TO USE: pick an item, rule on the design question it names, land the pin, **delete or
 demote the entry.** A register entry that survives a fix is a lie. **Do not add "needs
 review" language anywhere else in this file** -- put it here instead.
